## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a clear, repeated route: strategy overview, two segment reviews that follow the same template (overview, end markets, market pages, margin bridge), advanced composites, then targets, capital allocation and thesis. The blue and lime identity is consistent, section dividers (slides 1, 26, 39, 46, 54) and the tags 'Catalyze the core' and 'Build new platforms of scale' help orientation, and the slide 32 and 44 highlight pages tie each market page back to the trends. Weaknesses are material: slide 3 is an unreadable wall of legal text, several slides carry very small text (the captions on slides 12 and 13, the labels on slides 18, 36 and 43, the sources on slide 51), and some layouts are partly empty or washed out, such as the large blue gap on slide 13 and the faded text on slides 58 and 11. The reconciliation on slide 64 is tiny on a blank page, but that is a reference appendix.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the repeated segment template, the numbered margin bridges with lime driver tables (slides 36, 52, 59), the 'existing, evolving, emerging' flow diagrams, and the consistent colour coding and tag labels.

**Most useful repair:** Cut the legal text on slide 3 to a readable notice or move it to the appendix, enlarge the captions and diagram labels on slides 12, 13, 18, 36, 43 and 51, and enlarge or reflow the reconciliation table on slide 64.

**What would change the score:** I would reduce the stock-photo decoration and the repeated strategic-drivers pages (15, 38, 53) so more space goes to larger charts and the key numbers, and label the values in the bridge on slides 36 and 52 as slide 59 does.

_Confidence medium. 64 of 64 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a welcome slide and an agenda naming the presenters, followed by a forward-looking statements disclaimer and non-GAAP measures notice. The strategic overview comes next, covering the company's purpose, its growth framework of catalyzing the core and building new platforms of scale, and four strategic drivers: portfolio prioritization, amplified innovation, digital for operational excellence, and leadership and culture. Segment reviews follow for Color, Additives and Inks and for Specialty Engineered Materials, each giving an overview, end markets, addressable market sizes, and margin expansion levers. A spotlight on advanced composites comes next. The financial section covers sustainability commitments, long-term targets for organic growth and a path to 20%+ adjusted EBITDA margins, capital allocation priorities, and an investment summary. The deck closes with a reconciliation table of non-GAAP figures. Slides that show the structure well include the framework diagram on slide 10, the segment margin bridges on slides 36 and 51, and the consolidated margin bridge on slide 58.

## Questions about this deck

**What does the Avient investor day deck say about the 2023 sales of Avient's Color, Additives and Inks segment?**

Slide 27 states that Color, Additives and Inks had 2023 sales of $2.0B and describes it as #1 in industry. The slide also shows its 2023 sales split by region.

**How many slides does the Avient investor day deck have and how is it organized?**

The deck has 64 pages. Slide 2 lists the agenda, which covers the strategic overview, the Color, Additives and Inks and Specialty Engineered Materials segment strategies, the advanced composites spotlight, and the summary and financials.

**How does the Avient deck describe its growth framework for catalyzing the core and building new platforms of scale?**

Slide 10 shows the Catalyze the core approach with the label GROW BUSINESS AT GDP +. Slide 11 shows the Build new platforms of scale approach with the label GROW BUSINESS AT 10% PLUS CAGR.

**What adjusted EBITDA margin history does the Avient deck show?**

Slide 20 states that Avient's adjusted EBITDA margin rose from about 5% in 2006 to 16% in 2023. The slide ties this outcome to the company's evolution from a commodity portfolio and compounding business to a specialty formulator of materials.

**Layout**

Six equal-height vertical columns sit in a row across the lower-middle of the slide, starting at the left edge and running to the right edge with the sixth column cropped by the right edge; each column is a green card with a photo on top and text below. The large outlined title 'today...' sits top-left at the left margin, above the cards, with no title elsewhere. The footer runs along the bottom: the Avient logo at the left margin, the copyright lines beside it, and the page number '2' at the bottom right. Columns have narrow blue gutters between them; text is left-aligned inside each card.

**Visuals**

Six photographs of people, each a rectangular head-and-shoulders portrait placed in the upper part of its green card, full card width, with no overlay or tint. (1) Ashish Khandpur: smiling man with dark hair, navy blazer over light blue open-collar shirt, centred and cropped at mid-chest, against a blurred background of yellow, green and blue pattern, soft even lighting. (2) Woon Keat Moh: bald man with a slight smile, dark suit, white shirt and blue patterned tie, centred, against a plain grey backdrop, soft studio lighting. (3) Chris Pederson: man with grey hair, neutral expression, dark suit and white shirt, centred tightly, against a grey gradient backdrop, soft lighting with a shadow on the left side. (4) Mike Mosley: smiling man with grey hair, dark suit and white shirt, slightly right of centre, against a light grey backdrop. (5) Jamie Beggs: smiling woman with shoulder-length blonde hair, navy top, centred, against a plain grey backdrop. (6) Partly visible photo at the right edge: a classroom with a teacher at a green chalkboard and children seen from behind, several raising hands, wooden desks and chairs, warm natural light, cropped by the slide edge. One Avient logo in white at bottom left: stylised diamond-lattice mark followed by the bold wordmark AVIENT.

**Design**

Background: Solid bright blue #0095FF. Solid bright blue background (#0095FF) with a lime-green card system (#D5FF79) and no gradients, shadows or rounded corners. The title is an outline-only treatment with a light blue (#D5FF79-like pale blue, approximately #BFE9FF) stroke and no fill, in a geometric sans-serif typeface, lowercase, with the ellipsis set as three small squares. Labels SEGMENT STRATEGY: and SPOTLIGHT: are in bright blue (#0095FF) Arial Regular, capitals, about 1.9% of slide height. Footer text is white (#FFFFFF) Arial Regular at about 1.5% of slide height; the page number is white at about 2.2%. Card top bands are thin green strips above each photo. Title text is roughly 20% of slide height.

**Text**

Disclaimer
Forward-looking statements
Certain statements contained in or incorporated by reference into this presentation constitute “forward-looking statements” within the meaning of the Private Securities Litigation Reform Act of 1995. Forward-looking statements give current expectations or forecasts of future events and are not guarantees of future performance. They are based on management’s expectations that involve 2023, business risks and uncertainties, any of which could cause actual results to differ materially from those expressed in or implied by the forward-looking statements. You can identify these statements by the fact that they do not relate strictly to historic or current facts. They use words such as "will," “anticipate,” “estimate,” “expect,” “project,” “intend,” “plan,” “believe” and other words and terms of similar meaning in connection with any discussion of future operating or financial condition, performance and/or sales. items, include statements relating to future actions; prospective changes in raw material costs, product pricing or product demand; future performance; estimated capital expenditures; results of current and anticipated market conditions and market strategies; sales efforts; expenses; the outcome of contingencies such as legal proceedings and environmental liabilities; and financial results.
Factors that could cause actual results to differ materially from those implied by these forward-looking statements include, but are not limited to:
• Disruptions, uncertainty or volatility in the credit markets that could adversely impact the availability of credit already arranged and the availability and cost of credit in the future;
• The effect on foreign operations of currency fluctuations, tariffs and other political, economic and regulatory risks;
• Disruptions or inefficiencies in our supply chain, logistics, or operations;
• Changes in laws and regulations in jurisdictions where we conduct business, including with respect to plastics and climate change;
• Fluctuations in raw material prices, quality and supply, and in energy prices and supply;
• Demand for our products and services;
• Production outages or material costs associated with scheduled or unscheduled maintenance programs;
• Unanticipated developments that could occur with respect to contingencies such as litigation and environmental matters;
• Our ability to pay regular quarterly cash dividends and the amounts and timing of any future dividends;
• Information systems failures and cyberattacks;
• Amounts for cash and non-cash charges related to restructuring plans that may differ from original estimates, including because of timing changes associated with the underlying actions;
• Our ability to achieve strategic objectives and successfully integrate acquisitions, including the implementation of a cloud-based enterprise resource planning system, S/4HANA;
• Other factors affecting our business beyond our control, including without limitation, changes in the general economy, changes in interest rates, changes in the rate of inflation, geopolitical conflicts and any recessionary conditions; and
• Other factors described in our Annual Report on Form 10-K for the year ended December 31, 2023, under Item 1A, “Risk Factors.”
Any forward-looking statement speaks only as of the date on which such statement is made, and we undertake no obligation to publicly update forward-looking statements, whether as a result of new information, future events or otherwise. You are advised to consult any further disclosures we make on related subjects in our reports on Form 10-Q, 8-K and 10-K that we provide to the Securities and Exchange Commission.
Use of non-GAAP measures
This presentation includes the use of both GAAP (generally accepted accounting principles) and non-GAAP financial measures. The non-GAAP financial measures include: Adjusted Earnings Per Share, Adjusted EBITDA, Adjusted EBITDA margins and Adjusted free cash flow. Avient’s chief operating decision maker uses these financial measures to monitor and evaluate the ongoing performance of Avient and each business segment and to allocate resources.
A reconciliation of each historical non-GAAP financial measure with the most directly comparable GAAP financial measure is attached to this presentation which is posted on our website at www.avient.com.
Avient does not provide reconciliations of forward-looking non-GAAP financial measures, such as outlook for Adjusted EBITDA, adjusted Free Cash Flow and Adjusted Earnings Per Share, to the most comparable GAAP financial measures on a forward-looking basis because Avient is unable to provide a meaningful or accurate calculation or estimation of reconciling items, and the information is not available without unreasonable effort. This is due to the inherent difficulty of forecasting the timing and amount of certain items, such as, but not limited to, restructuring costs, environmental remediation costs, acquisition-related costs, and other non-routine costs. Each of such adjustments has not yet occurred, are out of Avient’s control and/or cannot be reasonably predicted. For the same reasons, Avient is unable to address the probable significance of the unavailable information.

### Slide 4

**Text**

Introduction / & strategic / overview
Ashish Khandpur
AVIENT INVESTOR DAY
December 4, 2024

**Layout**

Two-column split: a full-bleed photograph fills the left and centre of the slide, and a solid blue panel runs full height from about 65.2% to 96.3% of the width on the right. Within the panel, all text and the headshot are left-aligned to a common left edge at about x 66.7%. The title sits at the top of the panel from y 5.5%, the headshot and name label sit in the middle-lower area, and the event line and date sit at the bottom with a margin of about 3% from the base. There is no footer or slide number.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph (left, covering the whole slide behind the panel): a human hand with the index finger extended from the left edge, pointing toward a bright glowing orange-white light source at the centre-left. Around the light, many thin concentric elliptical rings and swirling streaks of light in blue, violet, white and orange radiate outward to the right, with scattered bokeh dots in blue, teal, red, yellow and purple. Composition: the hand enters from the left edge at mid-height, the light is about 45% across the slide, and the rings fill the middle of the image; the photograph is not cropped other than by the slide edges and is partly hidden by the blue panel. Lighting: dark, low-key, with the orange glow as the only warm highlight. Dominant colours: dark navy #0A1A33, black, electric blue, orange #F5A040. Overlay: none visible beyond the blue panel. Headshot photograph (boxed, rectangular, sharp corners): a smiling man with short dark hair, wearing a dark grey checked blazer over a light blue open-collar shirt with a white undershirt, facing the camera, head and shoulders framed in a medium close-up. Background is a blurred abstract wall of yellow, green and blue shapes. Lighting is soft and even, daylight-like. Dominant colours: mustard yellow, green, blue, dark grey. Not cropped beyond its box. Lime label rectangle: a solid lime-green bar directly beneath the headshot, same width as the photo, holding the name.

**Design**

Background: the photograph and the solid blue panel; no gradient, pattern or texture beyond the photo. Title: white #FFFFFF Arial Bold in sans-serif, regular sentence case, about 8.5% of slide height per line, tight leading of roughly 10% between lines. Body text: white #FFFFFF Arial Regular for the date, and Arial Bold for the event line, about 3% of slide height. Name label: black #000000 Arial Bold, about 3.3% of slide height, on a lime #D7FF7A bar. Accent colours: electric blue #0095FF panel, lime #D7FF7A label. Event line uses wide letter-spacing and all capitals. Shapes: the blue panel and lime bar have square corners, no border, no shadow, no gradient. The headshot has no border or shadow.

### Slide 5

**Text**

Great companies create value by...
making the whole / to be greater than / the sum of parts
balancing / the microscope / and the telescope
driving disciplined / processes to execute / and deliver results
Copyright © 2024 . / Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved
5

**Layout**

Three lime-green cards sit side by side in one row beneath a left-aligned title at the top left, with the middle card wider than the outer two. The cards span roughly 24% to 81% of the slide height, leaving a clear band above and below them. The Avient logo and copyright lines sit bottom left, and the slide number sits bottom right on the photograph. Left and right margins are about 2.2% of the slide width; the title's left edge is at 3.1%.

**Visuals**

Right half of the slide: a photograph of blue-tinted curved glass high-rise facades with a pale sky glow at top right and a grey-blue plaza with a curved walkway at bottom right, washed in blue so it reads as a background. Card 1 (left): a close-up photograph of watch movement gears, silver and gold, with ruby-coloured jewel bearings, sharp focus, lit from above, cropped to a rectangle, sharp corners, filling the card below its headline. Card 2 (middle): two side-by-side photographs. The left one shows a woman in safety glasses and a white lab coat with purple gloves looking into a microscope and holding a red-capped sample tube, with a blue-lit lab behind her. The right one shows a woman in a pink knitted hat and dark fur-trimmed jacket at a black tripod telescope at night, with a dark sky and ground behind her. Card 3 (right): a photograph of a Newton's cradle, polished chrome spheres in mid-swing against a dark grey background, with a reflective bright bar along the bottom and motion blur on the leftmost ball. The Avient logo is a white wordmark with a chevron-cross mark at bottom left. No charts or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF with a photograph of blue-tinted glass architecture covering the right half of the slide. Blue full-bleed background with a photographic layer on the right half. Title is white bold sans-serif (Arial Bold), about 5.9% of slide height, sentence case. Card headings are black bold sans-serif (Arial Bold), about 4.4% of slide height, with line pitch about 5.35% of slide height. Footer and page number are white regular sans-serif (Arial), about 1.5% and 2.2% of slide height. Cards are flat lime #D7FF7A rectangles with sharp corners, no border, no shadow. Photos sit flush inside the lower part of each card with a lime strip showing below them. No gradients on the cards.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

### Slide 6

**Text**

Key messages
Avient is evolving to
its next phase as an
innovator of materials
solutions to help our
customers succeed,
while enabling a
sustainable world
Our primary focus is on organic revenue growth
and margin expansion
► Our approach is to intersect secular trends and high growth markets with
our technologies to create product platforms of scale
► We have conducted extensive portfolio prioritization and identified growth vectors
both to catalyze growth in our core and to build businesses in high growth
markets supported by secular trends
► Margin expansion to be driven by higher volumes, profitable mix, productivity gains,
use of digital tools/processes, and a pipeline of differentiated products enabled by
hybridization of our technologies
Our strategy is enabled and driven by our focus on
(a) portfolio prioritization, (b) amplifying innovation, (c) digital for operational excellence
and growth, and (d) leadership, talent and culture for the Avient of the future
Copyright © 2024 .
Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved
6

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a full-bleed blue slide: a narrow full-height lime panel occupies the left column (about 3% to 29% of slide width, from the top edge down to about 89% of height) holding the title Key messages at top-left and a bold statement anchored at the bottom of the panel. The right column starts at about 33% of width and holds a two-line headline, three bullets and a closing strategy statement, all left-aligned with a ragged right edge. Footer runs along the bottom: AVIENT logo at far left, copyright block beside it, and page number 6 at bottom right, about 3% margin from the edges.

**Visuals**

none

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF with a lime #D5FF79 panel on the left. Flat, saturated blue background (#0095FF) with a lime (#D5FF79) rectangular panel on the left with square corners and no shadow or gradient. Left panel title and statement are black (#000000); right-column headline, bullet text and footer are white (#FFFFFF); bullet markers and the lime accent are #D5FF79. Typeface is Arial-style sans-serif (Arial Bold for headings and emphasis, Arial regular for body). Title Key messages is about 5.9% of slide height; right headline about 4.4%; bullets and strategy line about 3.0%; left statement about 3.7%; footer about 1.5%. Bold runs are used inside bullets for key phrases. Line spacing about 1.5 for bullets and about 1.5 for the left statement.

7

**Layout**

Full-bleed royal blue slide with a narrow full-height lime panel on the left (about 3 to 29 percent of width, 0 to 91 percent of height) holding the title and stat boxes, and a white content panel on the right (about 29 to 97 percent of width, 12 to 91 percent of height) with a blue-to-white gradient fading in from its left edge, holding two donut charts in the centre and a nine-row end-market list on the far right. The title 'Avient / at a glance...' sits top-left at about 5.7 percent from the top, left-aligned at about 4.6 percent from the left. A footer strip below the panels (about 94 to 97 percent of height) carries the white Avient logo at left, the copyright lines beside it and the page number 7 at far right near 96 percent of width.

**Visuals**

Chart 1, donut titled '2023 sales by business segment', centre at about 63 percent of width and 32 percent of height, outer diameter about 20 percent of width. Two segments: SEM (Specialty Engineered Materials) 36 percent, orange #FF7D41, spanning the upper-left arc; CAI (Color, Additives and Inks) 64 percent, blue #0095FF, spanning the lower and right arc. Labels printed in white bold on the segments: 36% and 64%. Segment labels in black at left: 'Specialty / Engineered / Materials' over bold 'SEM', and 'Color, / Additives / & Inks' over bold 'CAI'. Centre text reads '2023 sales' bold over 'by business segment' regular, on white. No legend box; labels sit beside the ring. Chart 2, donut titled '2023 sales by region', centre about 63 percent of width and 70 percent of height, same outer size. Four labelled segments printed in the chart: US, Canada 41 percent orange #FF7D41 (top and upper-right arc); EMEA 36 percent blue #0095FF (lower-right arc); Asia 18 percent khaki #B2A56D (left arc); LATAM 5 percent dark navy about #2E2B5F (small wedge at upper-left). White bold percentage labels on the segments: 41%, 36%, 18%, 5%. Black region labels outside the ring: 'US, / Canada' at upper right, 'LATAM' at left, 'Asia' at lower left, 'EMEA' at lower right. Centre text '2023 sales' bold over 'by region' regular. No legend box and no highlighted points. Chart 3, a list titled '2023 sales by end market' (bold '2023 sales', regular 'by end market') of nine rows, each with a rectangular photograph about 10 percent of width by 8 percent of height at left and the category name in black capitals with its percentage in bold blue #0095FF beneath: PACKAGING 23%, CONSUMER 19%, INDUSTRIAL 16%, TRANSPORTATION 10%, BUILDING & CONSTRUCTION 9%, HEALTHCARE 7%, DEFENSE 7%, ENERGY 5%, TELECOM 4%. Values are printed, so no estimates are needed. Photographs: PACKAGING shows a cluster of colourful plastic bottles and jugs in blue, green, yellow and red, bright daylight, flat front view, full-bleed in its cell. CONSUMER shows a smiling group of four people in winter clothing with shopping bags, warm light, the group filling the frame. INDUSTRIAL shows a worker in a yellow hard hat and orange vest looking at a tablet beside machinery with grey metal tones. TRANSPORTATION shows a close view of a car headlight or side mirror with orange light streaks and motion blur on a dark blue-teal road. BUILDING & CONSTRUCTION shows a crane and a partly built concrete and steel structure against a blue sky. HEALTHCARE shows gloved hands holding a green syringe near a blue backdrop. DEFENSE shows a grey military helicopter over desert terrain beside a soldier in camouflage, warm dusty tones. ENERGY shows high-voltage transmission towers and lines against a dark sky with warm sunset light. TELECOM shows a close view of glowing fibre-optic cables in red, orange and blue with light flare. All photos are cropped to the same rectangle, with no overlay or tint. Thin dotted blue separator lines run between rows at about 27, 35, 43, 51, 58, 66, 74 and 82 percent of height. Logo: the white Avient logo with its diamond-lattice mark and bold wordmark at bottom left. No other icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid royal blue #0095FF, with a lime #D4FF7A panel on the left and a white panel with blue-to-white gradient on the right. Flat, bold, high-contrast corporate look. Background royal blue #0095FF. Lime panel #D4FF7A with a black-tinted stat box system: dark olive #3B4120 boxes (black at 75 percent opacity over lime) hold white bold figures, and lighter olive #AACC62 boxes (black at 20 percent) hold black bold figures. Title and subtitle black #000000 in Arial Bold; title cap height about 5.9 percent of slide height, subtitle about 2.6 percent. Stat figures about 5.2 percent of height, labels in black Arial regular capitals about 2 percent. White content panel #FFFFFF with a blue-to-white gradient at its left edge. Chart colours: orange #FF7D41, blue #0095FF, khaki #B2A56D, navy about #2E2B5F. Accent percentages in blue #0095FF. Chart labels Arial regular black #000000, bold for headings and segment names, body about 2 percent of slide height. Footer copyright text Arial regular white about 1.5 percent of height. Shapes are square-cornered rectangles with no shadows, no rounded corners, and no outlines. Dotted separator lines are thin light blue.

### Slide 8

**Text**

The fast-changing world creating opportunities for us... / Our customers / need our help... / Auto interior carpet / manufacturer / meeting challenges arising / from changing regulations / Build market-centric / solutions / differentiated solutions bringing together all Avient / CAI / Color, UV durability / Lightweighting / Improved workability / Recyclability / SEM / Strength / Durability / Create new / businesses / at scale / there are / technology, / regulatory, / geopolitical and / societal / secular trends that are / creating high growth / markets... / 8 / Copyright © 2024 . / Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved

**Layout**

Full-width blue header band holds the title, flush left at about 3 percent margin and near the top (title top at 5.7 percent of height). Below it three equal-height columns sit side by side from 17.3 to 90.7 percent of height: a white column (0 to 39.8 percent width), a beige column (41.2 to 81.0 percent) and a narrow lime column (82.3 to 100 percent, bleeding to the right edge). Each column has a bold black heading at the top left. A thin footer row at the bottom holds the Avient logo and copyright at left and the page number at right (96.2 percent x, 94.4 percent y).

**Visuals**

Three photographs and one icon group. (1) Left white column, full-bleed photo of the rear cargo area of a car interior: a dark charcoal carpeted floor with a folded grey rear seat back on the left, a black trim panel rising in the centre, a silver door handle and grey door panel at upper right, a small round metal anchor bolt on the floor. Neutral daylight from upper left, dominant colours charcoal grey and silver, cropped to the column with a dark gradient shade over the lower part where the captions sit. (2) Middle beige column, photo of an outdoor composite deck: brown-grey wood-look boards, dark brown rails and posts with two post-cap lights, a teal patio umbrella over a round bistro table with two black wicker chairs with red cushions, a wicker sofa with colourful patterned cushions, a red throw, pink and yellow flowers in pots, a black lantern, a stucco house wall and pine trees behind. Warm late daylight, dominant colours brown, teal, tan and dark green, boxed to the column with a translucent dark band at the top behind the subtitle. Two callout boxes overlay the photo: a CAI box with four white bold bullets and an SEM box with two, each a translucent grey rectangle with a beige tab label and a downward wedge tail pointing to the right. (3) Lime column, three stacked chevron arrows at the bottom right in pale olive green, about #A8C95A, decreasing in opacity left to right. (4) EU flag in a small box in the left caption: dark blue field with a ring of twelve yellow stars. (5) Avient logo in white at bottom left: a diamond lattice mark beside the wordmark AVIENT.

**Design**

Background: Solid cobalt blue #0095FF covering the whole slide. Blue slide with a bright cobalt blue background, approximately #0095FF. Titles and headings are white (#FFFFFF) on blue for the main title and black (#000000) on the white and beige columns. Body text in the lime column is black #000000. Callout text and captions are white. Accent colours are lime #D8FF7A, beige #ECE6D9, grey #9D9391 at 85 percent opacity for callouts, and chevron olive about #A8C95A. Typeface is Arial Bold for titles, headings, captions and callout bullets, and Arial Regular for body text, copyright and page number; both are sans-serif. Main title is about 5.9 percent of slide height, column headings about 4.4 percent, body text in the lime column about 2.6 percent, callout bullets about 2.0 percent, footer text about 1.5 percent. Panels have square corners, no visible outlines and soft grey drop shadows around the white, beige and lime columns. Callouts have square corners and small wedge tails. No gradients other than the dark shades over the photos.

### Slide 9

**Text**

Intersecting secular trends and high growth markets with / our technologies to create product platforms of scale / Housing shortage / Increased power generation and distribution needs / driven by high performance computing and electric mobility / Heightened geopolitical tensions / Move to sustainability solutions / Tightening regulatory environment / Personalized, self-administered / healthcare solutions / AVIENT GROWTH VECTORS / Composites for building and construction / Composites for electrification and energy / Flame retardants / Composites for Defense and law enforcement / Non-PFAS functional additives / Plastic lightweighting / Engineered materials for healthcare / Drug delivery devices / Copyright © 2024 . / Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved / 9

**Layout**

Full-bleed slide with a single bold title across the top, left-aligned at about 3% margin and spanning two lines. Below, two columns: six pale-green arrow chevrons on the left (pointing right toward the right column) and a list of eight white text rows separated by thin white rules on the right, under a green pill label. The Avient logo sits bottom-left with the copyright block beside it, and the page number sits bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Six pale-green (#D7FF7A) right-pointing chevron shapes stacked vertically on the left, each with a notched right-hand point, containing black bold labels. Chevron 1: Housing shortage. Chevron 2: Increased power generation and distribution needs, with a second smaller grey line driven by high performance computing and electric mobility. Chevron 3: Heightened geopolitical tensions. Chevron 4: Move to sustainability solutions. Chevron 5: Tightening regulatory environment. Chevron 6: Personalized, self-administered healthcare solutions. On the right, a pale-green (#D5FF79) rounded pill label reading AVIENT GROWTH VECTORS with a small downward-pointing triangular tail at its lower left pointing at the first row. Below it, eight rows of white bold text, each followed by a thin white horizontal rule: Composites for building and construction; Composites for electrification and energy; Flame retardants; Composites for Defense and law enforcement; Non-PFAS functional additives; Plastic lightweighting; Engineered materials for healthcare; Drug delivery devices. The Avient logo (white stylised diamond-lattice mark with the word AVIENT in white bold) sits bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF. Solid bright blue background. Title in white bold sans-serif (Arial Bold style) at about 5.9% of slide height per line, lines spaced tightly. Chevron labels in black Arial Bold at about 3.0% of slide height. Right-column labels in white Arial Bold at about 3.0%. Pill label in black Arial Bold capitals with letter-spacing at about 2.0%. Copyright and page number in white Arial regular at about 1.5% and 2.2%. Accent colour pale lime green #D7FF7A for chevrons and pill. Subtitle line in dark olive grey about #4F5F2E. Thin white rules about 1px. Chevrons and pill have square corners except the pill which is fully rounded. No shadows or gradients.

### Slide 10

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Uses a two-part arrow framework with consistent labels to make the company's growth strategy easy to follow at a glance.

**Text**

Strategic approach to drive sustainable growth / Avient sales growth / Schematic illustration only / Catalyze the core / 1. Enhanced customer focus / 2. Solutions from across all Avient / 3. Eliminate complexity of portfolios, go-to-market models and organization / 4. Amplify innovation to grow double-digits in prioritized businesses through key growth vectors / 5. Digital for growth and operational excellence / 6. Share gains and geographic penetration / GROW BUSINESS AT / GDP + / Copyright © 2024 . / Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved / 10

**Layout**

Three zones under a full-width title at top left (x 3.1, y 5.7). Left: a boxed schematic graphic (x 3.1 to 26.1, y 18 to 86). Centre: a white column (x 33.6 to 48.6, y 18.4 to 86.4) with the heading Catalyze the core at top and GROW BUSINESS AT / GDP + anchored to its bottom. Right: a blue panel (x 48.6 to 96.9, y 18.4 to 86.4) with a thin white outline holding a six-item numbered list. Footer logo and copyright bottom left (y 94), page number bottom right (x 95.5, y 94.4). Left margin about 3 percent; text is left aligned throughout.

**Visuals**

One schematic illustration on the left, not a data chart. It is a vertical rectangle (about 23 by 68 percent of the slide) with a vertical gradient from dark blue #0063A8 at the top to white at the bottom left, fading to mid blue #0F6FB8 at the bottom right. A thick white arrow with a rounded arrowhead rises from the lower left (about x 3 y 58) to the upper right (about x 29 y 44), indicating an upward trend. A thin white horizontal line (about 2 px tall, x 8 to 34, y 65.8) runs from inside the gradient to a small white filled dot (diameter about 1.5 percent of slide width) at x 33.5 y 65.7 on the white column edge. The left graphic is boxed, not full-bleed, and carries the label Avient sales growth with Schematic illustration only. Two black 40 percent opacity highlight bars sit behind the text all Avient (x 71.0, y 28.1, 9.2 by 4.4 percent) and Amplify innovation (x 52.4, y 48.6, 19.5 by 4.4 percent). The Avient logo at bottom left is a white wordmark with a diamond-lattice mark.

**Design**

Background: Solid #0095FF bright blue, full slide. Solid bright blue background #0095FF. Title is white Arial Bold, all sentence case, about 5.9 percent of slide height. Heading Catalyze the core and GDP + are bold Arial in #0095FF. Body list is regular Arial in white #FFFFFF, about 3.3 percent of slide height, with numbers in regular Arial. Small label GROW BUSINESS AT is regular Arial caps in #0095FF at about 1.85 percent. Footer is white Arial about 1.5 percent. Highlight bars are black at 40 percent opacity, which renders as dark blue near #005CA3. The white column is #FFFFFF and the right panel is #0095FF with a 0.2 percent white border. No corner radii, no shadows, no gradients on the panels other than the left schematic. Overall look is flat and sans-serif.

### Slide 11

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are material: slide 3 is an unreadable wall of legal text, several slides carry very small text (the captions on slides 12 and 13, the labels on slides 18, 36 and 43, the sources on slide 51), and some layouts are partly empty or washed out, such as the large blue gap on slide 13 and the faded text on slides 58 and 11.

**Text**

Strategic approach to drive sustainable growth
Avient sales growth
Schematic illustration only
Build new
platforms
of scale
1. Play bigger and bolder in high growth markets and portfolios supported by secular trends to create product platforms of scale
2. Prioritize company-level growth vectors meeting the four-point criteria
MARKETS TECHNOLOGY CUSTOMER SCALE
3. Moving up the value chain, and expanding addressable market size by providing materials solutions in select and prioritized areas
GROW BUSINESS AT
10% PLUS CAGR
Copyright © 2024 . Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved
11

**Layout**

Full-bleed blue slide with the title across the top left at about 5.7% from the top and 3.1% from the left margin. Below the title, two columns sit between y 18.4% and 86.4%: a left illustration panel (x 3.1 to 26.1%) and a lime-and-blue content block (x 33.6 to 96.9%) split into a narrow lime column with the heading and a wider blue column holding a numbered list. A white Avient logo and copyright footer sit bottom left at y 94 to 99%, and the slide number 11 sits bottom right at x 95.6%, y 94.4%.

**Visuals**

Left illustration (schematic, not data-labelled): a rectangular gradient panel about 23% by 68% of the slide. Its top-left is deep blue (about #0063A6) brightening to #0095FF toward the right edge, and its bottom is white fading to mid blue (about #1A6FB0) at the bottom-right. A lime (#D6FE79) band rises from the lower-left in an S-curve to the upper right, filled with a lime-to-teal gradient, and ends in a lime arrowhead at about x 28%, y 21%. A white arrow runs from lower left to an arrowhead at about x 29%, y 44%. A thin lime horizontal line with a blue-ringed lime dot at its right end runs from the left panel at about y 38% toward the middle column edge at x 33.5%, fading out to the left. Four lime tags (#D8FF7A) with blue bold labels sit in a row under item 2, each a plain rectangle with square corners. Avient logo: white wordmark with a white diamond-lattice icon, bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF. Solid bright blue background (#0095FF) with white titles and body text, black text in the lime panel, and lime (#D7FF7A) accents. Title in Arial Bold, about 5.9% of slide height, white. Body text in Arial Regular about 3.3% of slide height, white. Heading in Arial Bold about 4.4%, black. Tag labels in Arial Bold about 2.6%, blue #0095FF. Footer text Arial Regular about 1.5%, white. Lime block has a thin lime border around the blue content block, square corners, no shadows. Left illustration has a smooth gradient with no border.

### Slide 12

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are material: slide 3 is an unreadable wall of legal text, several slides carry very small text (the captions on slides 12 and 13, the labels on slides 18, 36 and 43, the sources on slide 51), and some layouts are partly empty or washed out, such as the large blue gap on slide 13 and the faded text on slides 58 and 11.
- Suggested repair: Cut the legal text on slide 3 to a readable notice or move it to the appendix, enlarge the captions and diagram labels on slides 12, 13, 18, 36, 43 and 51, and enlarge or reflow the reconciliation table on slide 64.

**Text**

Catalyze the core – some examples today... / Healthcare / Drug delivery devices / Engineered materials / for healthcare / Functional additives / for lightweighting / Composites for / Defense and law / enforcement / in Color, Additives and Inks / presented by Woon Keat Moh / in Specialty Engineered Materials / presented by Chris Pederson / in Color, Additives and Inks / presented by Woon Keat Moh / in Specialty Engineered Materials / presented by Chris Pederson / AVIENT / Copyright © 2024 . / Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved / 12

**Layout**

Single-slide layout with the title in the top-left band at about 5.7% from the top and left margin about 3% of width. A light-blue 'Healthcare' pill sits below the title on the left, and a row of four equal-height photo cards (about 24% wide each, gaps about 2%) spans the slide width from the left edge to the right edge, with the rightmost card bleeding off the right edge. Each card has a bold white heading in its top-left corner and a two-line caption below it, aligned left at the card's left edge plus about 1%. The footer has the Avient logo at the bottom left, a copyright block beside it, and the page number at bottom right about 4.5% from the right edge.

**Visuals**

Four photographs in a row, each in a rectangular box with sharp corners and no border. (1) Drug delivery devices: a close-up of four pen-style injector devices lying diagonally, with green caps and purple/lavender bodies, on a teal-grey surface; soft focus at the top left; cropped at the card edges; cool, even daylight; dominant colours teal, green, lavender. (2) Engineered materials for healthcare: a smiling woman in an orange t-shirt with long dark hair, looking down while pressing a small grey wearable patch with a white dot onto her upper arm; beige sofa and teal cushion behind; warm indoor light; a light grey translucent gradient tint at the top of the image; dominant colours orange, beige, teal. (3) Functional additives for lightweighting: iridescent soap bubbles floating in front of green trees in daylight; bubbles show purple, pink and blue sheen; the top of the image has a dark grey translucent tint for the heading; dominant colours green, purple, pink. (4) Composites for Defense and law enforcement: a soldier in tan camouflage uniform and helmet with goggles, holding a rifle and a radio handset cable, seated at the right edge of the frame; a helicopter sits on a flat desert plain behind him; sandy brown ground under a pale sky; the image is cropped at the right edge of the slide; dominant colours tan, brown, grey-blue. A light blue rounded pill labelled Healthcare sits above the first two cards with a pale-to-blue vertical gradient, approximately 49% wide, showing no image.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF covering the whole slide. Solid bright blue background (#0095FF) with no pattern. Title and card headings are white (#FFFFFF) Arial Bold sans-serif at roughly 5.9% of slide height for the title and about 3.3% for card headings, with sentence case. Captions use white Arial Bold for the first line and white Arial regular for the presenter line, about 2.2% of slide height. The 'Healthcare' label is Arial Bold in blue #0095FF at about 4.4% of slide height, centred inside a pale-blue (#D6ECFF to #0095FF) rounded pill with a corner radius of about 1% of width. Photo cards have sharp corners, no shadow, no border. Copyright text is Arial regular white at about 1.5% of slide height. Accent colour is white on blue; no other accent colours.

**Layout**

Three equal-width image panels sit in a row across the middle of the slide, each with a bold title overlaid at its top-left and a two-line caption beneath it, on a solid blue field. The slide title runs across the top-left in one line, left-aligned at about 3% margin. The Avient logo sits bottom-left with a two-line copyright block to its right, and the slide number sits bottom-right. Panels are separated by gutters of about 2% of slide width, and captions are left-aligned to the inset of each panel.

**Visuals**

Three photographs in a row, each a plain rectangle with square corners and no border, shadow or tint. Left: a 3D render of glossy red and blue spheres joined by light grey sticks forming molecular models, on a pale blue-to-white gradient background with blurred red and blue dots, bright soft lighting, the model centred and framed loosely. Middle: a concrete multi-storey building under construction with exposed floor slabs, orange safety railings and scaffolding, a yellow tower crane lifting a pale wooden prefabricated module, a blue sky with white clouds and green trees at lower right; the photo is framed from low angle, cropped at the top. Right: steel lattice electricity pylons in dark silhouette against an orange and blue sunset sky with a bright sun glow at right, power lines and insulators visible, shot from low angle with the tower diagonal across the frame. Avient logo: white stylised diamond-lattice mark with the word AVIENT in white bold capitals, bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF. Solid bright blue background with no gradient or pattern. Titles are bold Arial-style sans-serif in white; the middle panel title is black. Captions are bold white sans-serif with a lighter regular white presenter line beneath. Title is about 5.9% of slide height, panel titles about 3.3%, captions about 2.2%, copyright about 1.5%. Photo panels are square-cornered rectangles with no outline. No accent colours beyond the photographs.

**Layout**

Full-bleed blue slide with a single-line title across the top at left (x 3.1%, y 5.7%), margin about 3% left. Below the title, three columns: a dark blue sales chart panel at left (x 3.1 to 27.2%, y 15.9 to 90.7%), a central callout column of numbered annotations and pill labels (x 30 to 58%), and a full-height lime panel at right (x 64 to 100%, y 16 to 90.7%) holding bulleted text. **Visuals**

Schematic bar chart titled 'Avient sales growth' labelled 'Schematic illustration only', with no axes, scale or printed values. Two white bars on a baseline, each capped with a lime-green gradient segment: bar 1 at x 6.1 to 11.2%, lime cap y 43.7 to 55.8%, white body to baseline; bar 2 at x 18.8 to 23.9%, lime cap y 29.1 to 50.1%, white body to baseline. Above bar 1 and bar 2 a lime arrow labelled '10% PLUS' runs diagonally upward to the right, from about x 11 y 55% to x 18.5 y 46%. Below it a pale blue-to-transparent arrow labelled 'GDP+' in #0095FF runs diagonally upward from about x 11.5 y 65% to x 18.5 y 58%. Two numbered circular markers: '2' (lime #D5FF79 circle with black bold number, at about x 30 y 31%) connected by a thin lime horizontal line to bar 2 at y 32.5%; '1' (white circle with #0095FF bold number, at about x 30 y 64.5%) connected by a thin white horizontal line to bar 2 at y 66%. Two pill-shaped labels: 'BUILD NEW PLATFORMS OF SCALE' in lime #D5FF79 fill, black bold, and 'CATALYZE THE CORE' in white fill, #0095FF bold. Footer logo: AVIENT wordmark with a stylised interlocking-chevron mark, white.

**Design**

Background: Solid colour #0095FF (bright blue) covering the whole slide. Solid bright blue background #0095FF. Left chart panel dark blue gradient from #0071C0 at top toward #0095FF at bottom, with a slight shadow. Lime accent #D5FF79 used for right panel, bar caps, arrow and pill. Title white #FFFFFF, Arial Bold (Helvetica-like sans-serif), approx 5.9% of slide height. Body text white #FFFFFF regular Arial/Helvetica sans-serif at 2.6% height. Right panel headings black #000000 Arial Bold at 3.3% height, body dark grey #333333 Arial regular, sub-bullets at about 2.6%. Bullet markers ► in #0095FF. Pill corners fully rounded. Connector lines thin, about 0.2% of height. No visible shadows on text; bars have a faint drop shadow.

### Slide 15

**Text**

Strategic drivers
To deliver sustainable growth and margin expansion
Portfolio prioritization
Prioritize to intersect growth and create space for investment
Amplify innovation
Innovate in prioritized areas to create differentiated product platforms of scale
Digital for operational excellence and growth
Use digital tools and processes to drive efficiencies and growth
Leadership, talent and culture for the Avient of the future
Harness the 9,000 minds of Avient by driving a culture of collaboration
AVIENT
Copyright © 2024 .
Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved
15

**Layout**

Four equal tall columns of the same width and height sit in a row across the middle of the slide, with a gutter of about 1 percent between them and left and right margins of about 1 percent. The title Strategic drivers sits top-left at about 6 percent from the top with a subtitle line beneath it, both left-aligned on a left margin of about 3 percent. A footer row at the bottom holds the Avient logo and copyright lines at left and the slide number 15 at right.

**Visuals**

Four rectangular photographs, one per column, each with a dark-to-colour gradient overlay that fades up from the bottom of the column. Column 1 (x 0.8): a dark hexagonal grid surface with a hand placing a gold coin on a stack of gold coins, warm tan and brown tones, lit from above, the hand entering from the upper right. Column 2 (x 25.6): a hand holding a glowing transparent light bulb with a glowing brain-pattern filament, set against a blue-to-magenta network of glowing dots and lines, dark navy and magenta tones. Column 3 (x 50.4): an aerial view of a container port at dusk with cranes and ships, overlaid with a semi-transparent network of connected nodes and icons and a central blue Wi-Fi symbol in a glowing ring, purple, blue and orange tones. Column 4 (x 75.2): close crop of rowers' oars and rowers' arms in navy kit on pale grey water, with a blue-grey tint at the bottom. No logos other than the Avient logo in the footer.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF covering the full slide. Solid bright blue background with white text throughout. Title in bold white sans-serif (Arial Bold) at about 5.9 percent of slide height. Subtitle in regular white Arial at about 3 percent. Column headings in bold white Arial at about 4.4 percent, with secondary lines in regular white Arial at about 3 percent. Body text in light grey-white Arial regular at about 3 percent, roughly 85 percent white. Accent colours come from the photographs: tan and brown, navy and magenta, purple and orange, slate blue. Photographs have square corners, no borders and no visible shadows; each column's bottom is tinted by a gradient from transparent to a dark tint (brown, rust, red-purple and slate blue respectively).

### Slide 16

**Text**

Portfolio prioritization & building new trajectories of growth
1
Differentially categorized product
portfolios at the company level
2
Identified growth vectors for
catalyzing the core and building
new businesses by playing
bigger and bolder
3
Making company-level
investments in select portfolios to
rapidly build businesses at scale
Portfolio view
Schematic illustration only
Launch
Growth
Maturity
Decline
SALES
TIME
Copyright © 2024.
Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved
16

**Layout**

Full-bleed blue slide with the title in one line across the top band, starting at x 3.9%. A tall lime panel occupies the left column (x 3.1 to 39.3%, y 0 to 90.7%) holding a three-item numbered list, and a boxed chart panel fills the right column (x 40.6 to 96.7%, y 21 to 79%). The footer sits along the bottom edge at y 94%, with the logo and copyright at left and the page number at right, x 95.5%.

**Visuals**

Schematic line-and-bubble chart in a white-outlined box, x about 40.5 to 97%, y about 21 to 79%. Type: schematic product life-cycle portfolio chart, no numeric values. Y axis is labelled SALES with an upward arrow at the left edge; X axis is labelled TIME with a rightward arrow along the bottom edge. Four phase columns separated by thin dark blue vertical rules are labelled Launch, Growth, Maturity and Decline. Blue translucent bubbles of varying sizes sit across the phases: several small bubbles cluster at the lower left near Launch, larger ones rise through Growth, the biggest sits in Maturity, and smaller ones cluster in Decline. A single lime-green (#D5FF79) S-shaped curve starts near the origin, rises steeply through Growth, peaks in Maturity, then falls with an arrowhead toward the lower right of Decline. A dashed lime curve branches from the peak, climbs past the Decline bubbles and ends in a faded arrow at the top right. Legend: none. Highlighted points: none. Chart labels 'Portfolio view' and 'Schematic illustration only' sit in the top-left corner of the box. Bubbles and curves are semi-transparent light and mid blue with white and lime strokes; the box is lit flat with no shadows.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF. Solid saturated blue background #0095FF with a lighter translucent white band behind the chart. Title is Arial Bold, sans-serif, about 5.9% of slide height for the cap-height line; the left title word is black #000000 on the lime panel and the right phrase is white #FFFFFF on blue. Body list is Arial regular, sans-serif, dark olive text about 3.6% of slide height with generous line spacing (about 5% per line). Numbers in the list are bold white on dark olive circles. Accents are lime #D5FF79 and blue #0095FF; phase labels are dark blue #005998 Arial Bold. Lines are white 0.3% stroke for the chart frame and axes, lime for the curves; corners are square except the pill-shaped SALES and TIME labels, which have rounded ends. No shadows or gradients.

### Slide 17

**Text**

Amplify innovation / Create value by amplifying and further strengthening our innovation muscle / EVOLVING / Building & construction composite panels / Fire-retardant composite panels / Next gen fire-retardant / UV resistant materials / Composite utility poles / 1 / Leverage rich technologies and / deep expertise from CAI and / SEM across portfolios / 2 / Hybridize technologies to / create unique and differentiated / pipeline of products / 3 / Consolidate back-end of product / innovation to create capacity / and capability in R&D / Copyright © 2024 . / Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved / 17

**Layout**

Three equal columns of cards, each 30% of slide width with about 1.9% gutters, spanning y 21.4% to 90.7%, with left and right margins of about 3.1%. The title sits top-left at x 3.1%, y 5.7%, with a subtitle line directly beneath it. The footer has the white AVIENT logo bottom-left, a two-line copyright block beside it, and the page number 17 bottom-right at the right margin.

**Visuals**

Card 1 (left): a photo mosaic in a 4-column by 4-row grid of tiles separated by thin lime (#D5FF79) gutters. Tiles include a black device on a green background, a red and white kitchen with cups, fibre-optic cable ends, grey-brown composite decking boards, white wall panels, a coiled cable or hose, a grey electrical cabinet with wires, a blue sky with a figure, orange and red juice bottles, a golden wheat field at sunset, paint cans in green, red and blue, rows of blue stadium seats, and a sunset electricity pylon. A large flat block arrow in lime #D2F76E runs diagonally from the lower-left (about x 9%, y 54%) up to the upper-right (tip about x 28.8%, y 29%) across the mosaic, with no transparency. Card 2 (centre): a flat diagram on a green gradient field (#0AA84F on the left fading to pale lime #B6F26A on the right) with a white curved left edge. Circular callouts are linked by dark green (#0B6B3A) arrows. Top-left circle shows light wall panels, labelled Building & construction composite panels. Top-right circle shows a building wall under blue sky, labelled Fire-retardant composite panels. The centre circle, on a black field, shows a red and blue flame swirl, labelled Next gen fire-retardant / UV resistant materials. Bottom-left circle shows grey steel tubes, labelled Composite utility poles. An arrow from the left edge enters the centre circle, a bent arrow branches from the centre to the top-right circle, and two long arrows run from the centre and the bottom-left circle to the right edge. The heading EVOLVING is at top-left in bold dark grey, and a cut-off letter E sits at the top-right edge. Card 3 (right): a close-up photo of a hand in a light blue-grey sleeve holding a ball-and-stick molecular model with red, white and black atoms, against a soft blurred blue background. Lighting is cool and diffuse, the subject is in sharp focus, and the frame is full-width and cropped at the card edges. Footer: the white AVIENT wordmark with a chevron-diamond mark.

**Design**

Background: Solid azure blue #0095FF covering the whole slide. Full-bleed saturated azure background. Title is white bold sans-serif (Arial Bold style) at about 5.9% of slide height. Subtitle is white regular sans-serif (Arial style) at about 3% of slide height. Card fills are lime #D5FF79 with a soft drop shadow beneath each card. Body text is dark grey #222222 regular Arial-style at about 3.3% of slide height, with key phrases in black bold. Number badges are solid black circles (about 2.2% wide) with lime #D5FF79 bold numerals at about 3% of slide height. Footer text is white regular Arial-style at about 1.5% of slide height. Shapes are square-cornered, with no visible outlines.

### Slide 18

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are material: slide 3 is an unreadable wall of legal text, several slides carry very small text (the captions on slides 12 and 13, the labels on slides 18, 36 and 43, the sources on slide 51), and some layouts are partly empty or washed out, such as the large blue gap on slide 13 and the faded text on slides 58 and 11.
- Suggested repair: Cut the legal text on slide 3 to a readable notice or move it to the appendix, enlarge the captions and diagram labels on slides 12, 13, 18, 36, 43 and 51, and enlarge or reflow the reconciliation table on slide 64.

**Text**

Amplify innovation
Building platforms for growth in composites for building and construction
EXISTING
Panels for Building & Construction
Thermoplastic sandwich panels
Decking reinforcements
Thermoplastic tapes
Color solutions for decking
Industry-focused color gamut
EVOLVING
Modular flame retardant panels
Custom composite panels
Color-stable, permanent anti-stat decking
Functional additives
FFT flake panels
Surface-modified reinforcements
EMERGING
Integrated flame-retardant panels
Multifunctional composite panels
Flame-retardant wood composites
Novel multi-material solutions
AVIENT
Copyright © 2024 .
Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved
18

**Layout**

Full-bleed three-band composition on a blue header and footer frame: a title and subtitle sit left-aligned at the top, and the content area is split into three zones, EXISTING (left white, about 0 to 50 percent width, with a house photograph), EVOLVING (central curved green band), and EMERGING (right beige panel). Each zone has a centered column heading near the top, with circular images and their labels stacked vertically. The AVIENT logo sits bottom-left with a copyright block beside it and the page number 18 at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Left: a large cutaway photograph of a two-storey house showing timber roof rafters, a chimney, interior rooms (kitchen, living area, staircase, brick walls and a dark front door) fading to white on its right edge, placed full height of the content band and cropped at the left slide edge. Circular images (about 10.5 percent width each) in the EXISTING zone: top, a green-grey sandwich panel stack with a cut edge showing a honeycomb core; middle, three stacked grey and white thermoplastic tape boards; bottom, four colour samples of decking boards in tan, beige, rust-red and brown. Centre (EVOLVING): top, a large grey panel assembly on scaffolding seen from above; middle, four dark wood-grain composite boards in charcoal and brown on a white circle; bottom, a white square panel standing in a pile of white flakes on a black circle. Right (EMERGING): top, a crane lifting a prefabricated wall module against a blue sky; bottom, a close-up of a grey-brown composite decking board with green plant leaves at left. Decorative thin arc lines frame the EXISTING circles. No charts, tables or logos besides the AVIENT wordmark.

**Design**

Background: Blue #0095FF header and footer frame with a white (#FFFFFF) and beige (#ECE2D8) central field and a green gradient band. Blue #0095FF header and footer bands with white title text; white and beige #ECE2D8 content field; green gradient band from about #00A84F at left to pale lime #D4FF6A at right. Title in bold Arial (sans-serif), white, about 5.9 percent of slide height. Subtitle in regular Arial, white, about 4 percent of slide height. Column headings EXISTING and EMERGING in bold Arial grey #7C7873 (EVOLVING white on green). Labels in bold Arial, dark grey #545859 on the white side and black #000000 on the green and beige sides, about 2.0 percent of slide height; descriptors in regular Arial, about 1.7 percent, grey #7C7873 or black. Circular images have thin white or light grey rims and soft drop shadows. Footer copyright in regular Arial, white, about 1.5 percent of slide height; page number regular Arial, white.

19

**Layout**

Full-bleed blue slide with the title Digital top-left, left-aligned at about 3% from the left edge and vertically centred in a header band of about 16% height. Below the title a row of four equal-height columns sits between y 16% and y 90.5%: a wide left panel (0 to 30% width) carrying a brain photograph with right-aligned text, then three narrow photo cards (about 22.5% width each, separated by gaps of about 1% width) with right-aligned titles at their top and white or black body text in a translucent-tinted lower panel. A footer band at the bottom holds the Avient logo at left (about 2% from the left edge), the copyright lines beside it, and the page number 19 at right (about 95.5%). The third and fourth cards run to the right slide edge area, with the fourth card extending to the right edge at about 99.9%.

**Visuals**

Four photographs and one logo. (1) Left panel, x 0 to 30%, y 16 to 68.2%, boxed and not full-bleed vertically: a dark navy-to-black photograph of a female head in profile facing right, with a glowing neural network of thin white lines and dots; the brain is coloured in cyan and blue at top, red and magenta at right, and gold-yellow at lower left, with a bright blue-white glow. Lighting is dark and low-key with the illumination coming from the brain itself. Framing is a tight head-and-shoulders profile crop. (2) Card two, x 30.9 to 53.3%, y 16 to 67.9%, boxed: a bearded man in a white hard hat, dark-rimmed glasses, and a yellow-and-navy high-visibility jacket, looking down at a black tablet held in both hands in an industrial interior with blurred warm bokeh lights. The photo is tinted yellow-olive across its lower part by the overlay. (3) Card three, x 54.1 to 76.6%, y 16 to 67.9%, boxed: a close-up of a hand holding a small clear measuring cup over a tablet displaying colour swatch fans in red, orange, blue, green and pink, with coloured swatch chips and pens around it; cool blue and white lighting, with a diagonal composition from upper left to lower right. (4) Card four, x 77.4 to 100%, y 16 to 67.9%, boxed and running to the slide edge: a white humanoid robot head in profile at left with a mechanical ear and eye detail, a robot arm reaching toward a laptop at lower right, with teal icon circles (a laptop, a document and a graduation cap) and white handwritten-style equations on a light grey-blue background with a soft pink-white glow at top. Logo: the AVIENT wordmark with a diamond-lattice icon at x 2.3, y 94.1, w 7.5, h 3.2, white on the blue background.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF covering the whole slide. Bright blue background #0095FF with no gradient on the base. Titles are white #FFFFFF Arial Bold, large; the card titles are white on the yellow and blue cards and black #000000 on the white card. Body text is Arial regular, black #000000 on the yellow and white areas and white #FFFFFF on the blue area. Accent labels EXPECTED BENEFITS are set in Arial Bold, letter-spaced capitals, in yellow #C5BE38 on the yellow card, white on the orange and dark tabs. Orange tab fill #E8572A on card three. Card lower panels are semi-transparent colour washes: yellow-olive #C8C23F fading to a darker olive at the bottom on card two, a steel-blue gradient #2E7AA8 to #1F5F8B on card three, and a grey-to-white gradient on card four. Title size is about 5.9% of slide height for the slide title and about 3.7% for card titles; body text is about 2.6% and the labels about 1.85%. Shapes have square corners, no visible borders and no shadows.

20

**Layout**

Full-bleed blue slide with the title top-left at about 3% left margin and 6% from top, left-aligned. Three horizontal chevron bands form a left-to-right process flow across the vertical middle (about 31% to 74% of height): a pale left chevron labelled Commodity portfolio and compounding, a larger centre chevron labelled Specialty formulator of materials, and a right rectangular band with an arrow-point cut-out holding the OUTCOME text. A small ENABLERS label with two lime pills sits above the left-centre, with a curved lime arrow linking them to the centre chevron. Footer: AVIENT logo and copyright lines bottom-left, page number bottom-right at about 95.5% left, 94.4% top.

**Visuals**

Process diagram with three horizontal chevron shapes and one curved arrow. (1) Left shape: pale blue chevron, rectangular left portion with a right-pointing arrow tip, left edge at slide edge, horizontal gradient from light blue #8FD0FF at the left to near-white #EAF6FF at the right, label Commodity portfolio and compounding in blue text right-aligned. (2) Centre shape: larger chevron with notch on left and point on right, horizontal gradient from light blue at left to near-white #F2F9FF, label Specialty formulator of materials centred in blue. (3) Right shape: rectangular band extending to the slide's right edge with a notch on its left edge, fill light blue #B0DDFF at about 67% opacity, holding OUTCOME and the outcome statement in black. (4) Enablers: two adjacent lime green pills, #D5FF79, joined at a thin white/blue divider, first labelled Acquisitions, second labelled Divestitures, dark text. (5) Connector: a curved arrow in lime #D5FF79 with a soft gradient fading from blue-green at its start, starting under the Acquisitions/Divestitures divider, curving down and right, ending in a triangular arrowhead pointing right into the centre chevron's upper-left area, with a faint drop shadow. No photographs or icons apart from the logo.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF. Solid bright blue background #0095FF with no gradient on the slide itself. Title in white Arial Bold (sans-serif, bold) at about 5.9% of slide height. Chevron labels in blue #0095FF Arial Bold, centre label about 4.4% of slide height; outcome statement in black Arial regular about 3.3%; OUTCOME label in blue #0095FF Arial Bold capitals about 3.0%. Enabler pills in lime #D5FF79 with dark #1A1A1A Arial Bold text; ENABLERS heading in light blue-white #E6F4FF Arial Bold capitals about 2.2%. Chevrons use gradient fills (blue to white) and the right band is semi-transparent light blue; shapes have square edges and no outlines. Lime arrow has soft drop shadow. Footer text white Arial regular about 1.5%.

**Layout**

Full-bleed blue slide with a single-line title at top left (x 3.1%, y 5.7%) and a three-stage left-to-right chevron process occupying the middle band, with the largest chevron on the right. The Avient logo sits bottom left at x 2.3%, with the copyright block beside it and the slide number at bottom right (x 95.5%, y 94.4%). Left margin is about 3% and the bottom footer band sits at about 94% to 97% of the height.

**Visuals**

Three horizontal chevron (arrow-shaped) process shapes read left to right: a pale blue chevron labelled Commodity portfolio and compounding, a larger pale blue chevron labelled Specialty formulator of materials, and a large lime-green chevron labelled Innovator of materials solutions with a subline. Above them, a label ENABLERS sits over two translucent white rounded pills labelled Acquisitions and Divestitures. A curved blue arrow with a gradient fades from the pills down and right into the Specialty chevron, pointing right with an arrowhead. The Avient logo (stylised interlocking diamond mark plus AVIENT wordmark in white) sits at bottom left. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background: Solid azure blue #0095FF. Solid saturated azure background with white title and white footer text. Chevron fills use a light-blue to pale-blue horizontal gradient (about #9FD8FF to #D0EBFF) with flat azure edges; the green chevron is flat lime #D7FF79. Title is bold Arial/Helvetica sans-serif, about 5.9% of slide height in cap-height terms. Chevron labels are bold sans-serif in mid blue #4DB3FF. Innovator heading is bold black Arial, about 5.0% of height; subline is regular black Arial about 2.6%. Footer text is regular Arial about 1.5%. Shapes have sharp corners, no shadows, no outlines except thin white hairlines at the junctions between chevrons. Pills are rounded with large corner radius and white at 60% opacity.

### Slide 23

**Text**

OUR / PURPOSE / Innovator of materials solutions to help our customers / succeed, while enabling a sustainable world / STRATEGIC / APPROACH / Intersecting high growth markets and / secular trends with our technologies to / create product platforms of scale / Catalyze the core / GROWING / AT GDP+ / Build new platforms of scale / GROWING / AT 10% PLUS CAGR / STRATEGIC / DRIVERS / Portfolio / prioritization / Amplify / Innovation / Digital / for Operational Excellence / and Growth / Leadership, talent / and culture / for the Avient of the future / FOUNDATIONAL / STRENGTHS / Unwavering customer focus / Global reach with a local touch / Diverse / technology portfolio / Commercial excellence, / financial rigor & prudence / Culture of / safety and sustainability / Copyright © 2024 . / Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved / 23

**Layout**

Full-slide blue field with a left label column (right-aligned white small caps labels, each preceded by a white ▶ marker) at about x 1.7 to 14 percent. Four horizontal bands run to the right at x 12.2 to 98.5 percent: a white rounded title box at the top (y 3.1 to 19.1), a dark navy strategic approach band (y 20.2 to 38.9) split into a left statement and two right arrow-shaped bars, a row of four equal photo tiles (y 40 to 75) with titles overlaid at the bottom, and a row of four equal foundational strip boxes (y 76.1 to 85). The title is left-aligned at the top of its box and the labels align to each band's top. Footer: Avient logo bottom-left with copyright text beside it at about y 94; page number '23' bottom-right at x 95.5 percent.

**Visuals**

Four photographs in a row, each boxed (not full-bleed) with square corners, equal width about 21 percent of slide width, same top edge y 40 percent. Photo 1 (Portfolio prioritization): a hand from the upper right places a gold coin on a stack of gold coins on a dark grey surface patterned with raised hexagons; warm gold and charcoal tones; the tile is cropped to show the hand top right and coins centre; a dark brown-to-transparent gradient overlay darkens the bottom third for the white text. Photo 2 (Amplify Innovation): two hands cup a glowing clear light bulb containing a bright blue illuminated brain; behind it a dark background with a network of glowing cyan and magenta dots joined by thin lines; cool blue and magenta palette; a dark navy gradient overlays the bottom for the text. Photo 3 (Digital for Operational Excellence and Growth): an aerial dusk view of a container port with lit cranes, stacked containers and water, overlaid with a blue translucent network graphic; a glowing blue circle with a white Wi-Fi symbol sits centre, and small white-outlined icons (cloud, lock-like symbol, camera-like squares, cog) sit at the corners and edges; purple-blue tint with a dark gradient at the bottom. Photo 4 (Leadership, talent and culture): close crop of rowers in dark sportswear gripping black oars with yellow-tipped handles, shown diagonally against a bright white sky; the top of the image is light, and a dark slate-blue gradient overlays the bottom third for the white text. Logo: Avient wordmark with a white geometric diamond-lattice mark, white on blue, bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Solid colour #0095FF (bright azure blue) covering the whole slide. Solid bright blue background #0095FF. Title is set in Arial Bold, blue #0095FF, on white. Body and labels are white Arial, with black Arial Bold for the bar text. Accents: lime #D5FF79 bar. Strategic approach statement is white bold sans-serif (Arial Bold) at about 3.7 percent of slide height. Title is about 5.2 percent of slide height, Arial Bold. Labels are about 2.0 percent of slide height, regular weight, uppercase. Foundational headings are about 2.2 percent, bold; their subtext about 2.0 percent, regular. Photo titles are about 3.7 percent bold white with a 2.6 percent regular subline. Shapes: the title box has rounded top-right and top-left corners of about 1.5 percent radius; the dark navy panel is a rectangle with a subtle vertical gradient from #0B3A66 at left to #0A4D8A at right; the white bar is #FFFFFF and the lime bar #D5FF79, both with a pointed right end (arrow shape) about 2 percent deep. Foundational boxes are slate-blue #4A5C8C fading to #2E3F6E at the bottom with a soft gradient and rounded outer bottom corners. No shadows.

### Slide 24

**Text**

Redesigning organizations & compensation to drive strategy / CAI / Colors, / Additives / and Inks / • Reorganized under a single global leadership for the segment / • Streamlined organization and eliminated structure and complexity to serve customers more effectively / • Disciplined sales processes, cadence, and “play to win” mentality / SEM / Specialty / Engineered / Materials / • Dedicated and focused organizations for growth vectors as needed / • Making up front focused investments in people, capex, R&D and commercialization capabilities / R&D / Research and / Development / • Consolidated R&D expertise from businesses to resource and develop opportunities for all Avient, and to provide a pipeline of solutions to our customers, especially in prioritized areas for growth / • New CTO hired / New business / development / & marketing excellence / • New SVP hired / Compensation / • Designed a new compensation plan rollout in 2025 / • Links more directly to our strategy execution and objectives of top line and bottom-line growth / Copyright © 2024 . / Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved / 24

**Layout**

Full-bleed blue slide with the title running across the top at left margin x≈3%, y≈6%. Below it a grid of white boxes: a three-row left block (CAI, SEM, R&D rows; the R&D row is split into a wide R&D panel and a narrower New business development panel) spanning x≈3% to 74%, with a lime Compensation column at right spanning x≈75% to 97% from y≈15% to 89%. Left-aligned text throughout with a consistent ≈3% margin; the footer sits at the bottom with the Avient logo and copyright at left and the page number 24 at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Logo: Avient wordmark with a stylised diamond-lattice mark in white, bottom left at about x 2.3%, y 94.1%, w 7.5%, h 3.2%. No charts, photographs or illustrations. The small rasterised image boxes on the slide are copies of the text lines they sit on (bullet lines, row labels and bold phrases) and carry no separate visual content.

**Design**

Background solid bright blue #0095FF. Panels are white #FFFFFF rectangles with square corners and no visible borders or shadows, separated by blue gutters of about 1% of slide width. The Compensation panel is lime #DAFF7A. Titles: white bold sans-serif (Arial Bold / Helvetica Bold style) at about 5.9% of slide height for the main title. Section headers CAI, SEM, R&D and New business development are bright blue #0095FF bold sans, about 5.2% (and 4.4% for New business development). Sub-labels are blue #0095FF regular sans at about 2.2%. Body bullets are black #000000, Arial regular with bold phrases, about 2.6% of slide height. Compensation heading is black bold at 4.6%. Footer text is white regular at about 1.5%, page number white at 2.2%. Line spacing for bullets is generous (about 1.6 times the font size).

**Text**

Strong and committed team leading the change / ALSO HERE / TODAY / NEW / NEW / Woon Keat Moh / Chris Pederson / John Midea / Jamie Beggs / Amy Sanders / Phil Clark / SVP and President / SVP and President / SVP, Global Ops and / SVP and / SVP, General Counsel / SVP and / Color, Additives and Inks / Specialty Engineered / Process Improvement / Chief Financial Officer / and Secretary / Chief Technology Officer / Materials / ALSO HERE / TODAY / NEW / NEW / Mike Irwin / Kristen Gajewski / Joel Rathbun / Leslie Sequeira / Kyle Rose / SVP, New Business / SVP and Chief / SVP / Chief Information Officer / VP, Corporate / Development and / Human Resources / Mergers & Acquisitions / Communications / Marketing Excellence / Officer / Copyright © 2024 . / Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved / 25

**Layout**

Title sits top-left at about 3% left margin, spanning roughly three quarters of the width, with the content below in a grid of eleven people: six columns in the first row (x about 3, 19, 36, 52, 68, 84 percent) and five columns in the second row aligned to the first five columns, leaving the last grid cell empty. Each person block is a photo above a bold name and a regular-weight role text, all left-aligned to the photo edge. Footer: Avient logo bottom-left at about 2% left margin with copyright text beside it, and page number 25 bottom-right near the right margin.

**Visuals**

Eleven business headshots, head and shoulders, cropped square and placed in boxes about 10.5 by 18.7 percent of the slide: Woon Keat Moh (man, dark suit, smiling, grey studio backdrop); Chris Pederson (man, dark suit, neutral expression, charcoal backdrop); John Midea (man with wire-rimmed glasses, dark suit, smiling, grey backdrop); Jamie Beggs (woman with shoulder-length blonde hair, smiling, grey-beige backdrop); Amy Sanders (woman with blonde bob, smiling, light grey backdrop, orange frame and orange NEW badge); Phil Clark (man, dark suit, smiling, light grey backdrop, orange frame, dark navy ALSO HERE TODAY tag and orange NEW badge); Mike Irwin (man with short grey hair, navy suit, smiling, orange frame, orange NEW badge, ALSO HERE TODAY tag); Kristen Gajewski (woman with long blonde hair, smiling, grey backdrop); Joel Rathbun (bald man, dark suit, smiling, grey backdrop); Leslie Sequeira (man with dark curly hair, dark suit, smiling, orange frame, orange NEW badge); Kyle Rose (man with short dark hair, dark suit, slight smile, grey backdrop). Lighting is soft, even and frontal on all portraits. Portraits are full-square crops with no overlay, except the orange frames. Orange rectangular frames about 0.5 percent thick surround Amy Sanders, Phil Clark, Mike Irwin and Leslie Sequeira. Orange circular NEW badges, about 4 by 6 percent, sit at the top-right corner of Amy Sanders, Phil Clark, Mike Irwin and Leslie Sequeira, overlapping the frame corner. Two dark navy tags labelled ALSO HERE TODAY sit above the top-right photo (Phil Clark) and above the bottom-left photo (Mike Irwin). A white Avient logo with the wordmark sits bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF. Flat solid bright blue background #0095FF with no gradient or texture. Title white #FFFFFF Arial Bold, sentence case, about 5.9 percent of slide height, one line. Names white #FFFFFF Arial Bold about 2.2 percent of height. Role text white #FFFFFF Arial regular about 1.85 percent of height, line spacing tight (about 1.1). Accents: orange #F26B3B for photo frames and NEW badges with white NEW text; ALSO HERE TODAY tags dark navy #0A4C78 with white Arial Bold capitals and wide letter-spacing, about 1.85 percent. Badges have soft drop shadows; tags carry a faint black 50 percent opacity shadow. Frames are square, no corner radius. Copyright text Arial regular about 1.5 percent. Page number Arial regular white about 2.2 percent.
