### Slide 26

**Review notes**

- Review: The blue and lime identity is consistent, section dividers (slides 1, 26, 39, 46, 54) and the tags 'Catalyze the core' and 'Build new platforms of scale' help orientation, and the slide 32 and 44 highlight pages tie each market page back to the trends.

**Text**

Color, / Additives / and Inks / SEGMENT STRATEGIC REVIEW / Woon Keat Moh / AVIENT INVESTOR DAY / December 4, 2024

**Layout**

Two-zone layout: a full-bleed photograph fills the left and right edges, with a solid blue panel covering the right third of the slide from top to bottom. The title sits top-left of the panel at about 4.5% from the top, followed by the subtitle, a portrait with a name label, and the event name and date stacked at the bottom. All panel text is left-aligned to a common left edge at about 66.7% of slide width, with no footer or slide number.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph behind everything: a dark hand with an extended index finger touches a bright orange-white glowing point at the centre-left; from that point, concentric elliptical rings of thin blue, violet, white and orange light sweep up and to the right, scattered with bokeh dots in blue, red, yellow, green and purple. Lighting is a single hot glow against a near-black navy background. The hand enters from the left edge and is cropped by the left edge. The photo is covered on its right third by the blue panel. Portrait photograph of a bald, smiling East Asian man in a dark suit, white shirt and patterned blue tie against a flat grey backdrop, head and shoulders framed in a plain rectangle, boxed and not cropped at the edges, with soft even studio lighting. It sits above a lime label bar carrying the name.

**Design**

Background is a dark navy-to-black photograph on the left and right edges, with a solid bright azure panel #0095FF on the right third. Title text is white #FFFFFF in Arial Bold (Helvetica-like grotesque sans-serif), about 8.9% of slide height per line, with tight leading. Subtitle is white #FFFFFF in Arial Regular, all caps, widely letter-spaced, about 2.6% of slide height. Name label is black #000000 Arial Bold about 3.3% of height on a lime #D7FF7A rectangle with no border or radius. Event name is white #FFFFFF Arial Bold, all caps, widely letter-spaced, about 3.0% of height. Date is white #FFFFFF Arial Regular about 3.0% of height. Shapes are sharp-cornered rectangles with no shadows or gradients; the photograph carries no overlay other than the panel covering it.

28

**Layout**

Full-slide blue field with the title top-left at about 4.6% from the left, in a single row near the top. Below it a wide lime panel spans the slide width (about 3% to 97%) holding a left badge, a bold statement and a right bullet list separated by a thin blue vertical rule; beneath it a three-part band holds a navy sales circle at left, a donut chart at centre and a 3-by-2 photo grid with captions under 'KEY END MARKETS' at right. Footer logo sits bottom-left and the page number bottom-right, both at about 94% height.

**Visuals**

Donut chart titled '2023 sales by region' with centre label '2023 sales / by region'. Four segments, clockwise from top: US, Canada 34% (orange #FF7D41, top right of ring); Europe, Middle East, Africa 37% (blue #0095FF, right and bottom); Asia 21% (khaki #B2A56D, bottom left); Latin America 8% (navy #2C2E5B, left). Percentages printed in white bold on each segment except US, Canada, whose label sits outside. Values are printed, no axes, no legend; no highlighted points. Navy filled circle (#2C2E5B) reading '2023 SALES' over '$2.0B' as a KPI. Circular badge at top left: blue (#0095FF) disc with a ring of small lime (#D5FF79) dots, a lighter top highlight, '#1' in large white bold and 'IN INDUSTRY' in white, slightly overlapping the ring. Six rectangular photographs in a 3-by-2 grid, each about 9.7% wide by 13.3% high, unrounded, not cropped to shape, sharp edges: PACKAGING (colourful plastic bottles and jugs in yellow, green, blue and white, lit evenly); CONSUMER (snowboarder in blue jacket and yellow board jumping with spray of snow, blue sky); HEALTHCARE (close-up of green-capped plastic syringes or pens in grey, white and purple, shallow focus); BUILDING & CONSTRUCTION (tower crane and steel scaffolding at sunset, orange and blue sky, warm backlight); INDUSTRIAL (blue robotic arm with glowing digital interface, dark background); TRANSPORTATION (interior of car with orange sunlit light flare and blurred road, teal and orange tones). Small logo: white AVIENT wordmark with diamond icon, bottom-left. Several small embedded image objects (144 by 45, 132 by 44, 46 by 44 pixels) sit over the badge and sales circle without visible content of their own. Lime-to-blue gradient bands are embedded 900 by 260 pixel images.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF covering the whole slide.

**Layout**

Full-bleed blue slide split into a title band across the top and a four-column row below: a large photo column on the left and three equal-width text cards on the right, the last card running off the right edge. The title 'Our value' sits top-left at about x 3.1% and y 5.7%, left-aligned. The footer runs along the bottom with the Avient logo at bottom-left and copyright text beside it, and the page number 29 sits bottom-right. Column tops align at y 16% and the photo and cards share a common bottom edge near y 90.8%.

**Visuals**

Four photographs and three small green pointer triangles. (1) Left column, photo of a ColorWorks showroom interior: a white countertop with white and orange bar stools in the foreground, a large colourful abstract swirl mural on the back wall at left, rectangular linear ceiling lights and exposed dark ceiling beams, a shelf of colourful paint or fabric samples at right, a potted plant, a polished light-grey floor. Cool white light with warm red and orange accents; photo is cropped to a tall rectangle, boxed, not full-bleed. (2) Card 'we know color' photo: a man's hand holding a tablet showing a red colour swatch, against a wall of colourful sample displays and shelves of yellow, orange and blue colour cards; the lower part of the photo fades into a taupe-grey gradient overlay. (3) Card 'we are global' photo: a glowing blue network illustration over a city skyline and world map, with connected circular person icons, chat bubbles, envelope, camera and location pin icons linked by lines; the lower part fades into a dark teal overlay. (4) Card 'we innovate' photo: an aerial view of dense green forest canopy with a light-blue world map cut out of it; the lower part fades into an olive-green overlay. Each photo is a rectangle with square corners and no border. Three downward-pointing triangles in lime #D5FF79 sit under the header bars of the three cards, overlapping the top edge of each photo. An Avient logo (blue-and-white diamond mark with wordmark) sits bottom-left at about x 2.3% y 94.1%.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF covering the whole slide. Solid bright blue background #0095FF throughout. Title 'Our value' in white (#FFFFFF) Arial Bold, about 5.9% of slide height. Card headers are lime #D5FF79 bars with black (#000000) Arial Bold text about 3.7% of slide height. Card body figures are white Arial Bold at about 5.9%; labels are white Arial regular at about 2.6% with wide letter-spacing. Footer text is white Arial regular about 1.5%; page number about 2.2%. Photos have square corners and no shadows; the card bottoms are darkened by gradient overlays from transparent at the photo midpoint to dark tints at the bottom (taupe-grey, teal, olive). Gutters between columns are about 0.8% wide and blue shows through them.

**Text**

**Layout**

Full-bleed blue slide with the title top-left at about 3% left margin and 6% from top. Below it a lime-green band spans the full width holding four equal photo tiles in a row (columns at about 1.4%, 26.1%, 50.8% and 75.3% from left, each about 23% wide, top 18.8% and 33.4% tall), each with a caption bar near its bottom edge. Beneath the band a white panel (3.1% to 96.9% horizontally, 55% to 90.8% vertically) holds four columns of blue pill buttons, one column per photo, aligned left-to-centre under the tiles, with small green pointer triangles linking each photo to its column. The footer row has the Avient logo bottom-left and copyright text beside it, and the page number bottom-right at about 95.5% from left and 94.4% from top.

**Visuals**

Four photographs in a horizontal row, each a rectangular box with square corners, sitting on the lime-green band. Tile 1 (left, labelled Colorants): many colourful plastic bottles and containers in red, orange, purple, pink, teal and yellow, with a wooden-handled brush at lower right, lit evenly from the front on a light grey background, shown in close framing. Tile 2 (labelled Additives): flat-lay on white of five glass bottles of juice in yellow, red, green, yellow and dark magenta in a row, surrounded by sliced citrus (orange, grapefruit, lime, lemon), kiwi halves and spinach leaves, bright even top light. Tile 3 (labelled Screen printing inks): close view of an orange jersey with a large white block letter pattern reading TEXAS in outlined form, a white helmet and shoulder pad at upper edge, warm orange and white dominant colours, slightly cropped at the top. Tile 4 (labelled Specialty coatings / Advanced dispersions): outdoor playground with blue and pink canopy roofs, blue poles, yellow and green slides and a green panel with coloured letter blocks, under a pale blue sky, with a sandy ground; dominant blue, yellow and green. Each photo has a dark translucent overlay bar (black at 40% opacity) along its bottom edge carrying the white caption. Logo: Avient wordmark with a white geometric diamond-lattice icon at bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF (fill covers full slide, with a white panel and green band on top). Solid bright blue background #0095FF. Title is white bold sans-serif (Arial Bold) at about 5.9% of slide height. Lime-green band #D5FF79 with the photos set within it. White panel #FFFFFF with square-cornered edges and pill buttons #0095FF with rounded fully-curved ends. Captions and pill labels are white Arial Bold at about 2.6 to 3.0% of slide height. Footer text is white Arial regular at about 1.5% of slide height; page number white Arial regular at about 2.2%. Pointer triangles are lime green #D5FF79 with a soft grey drop shadow beneath the band and panel edges. Photos have soft shadows.

**Layout**

Full-bleed blue slide with the title across the top left at about 5.7% from the top and a 3.1% left margin. The body is two columns: a tall gradient chart panel on the left (x 3.1 to 26.1%, y 18.3 to 86.2%) and two stacked boxes on the right (x 33.6 to 96.9%), the upper lime box at y 18.4 to 48.2% and the lower white box at y 50.0 to 86.4%. Each right box holds a text label at the left and three photo tiles at the right in a 3-column strip (x 48.8, 64.5, 80.2%, each 15.7% wide). The footer has the Avient logo and copyright at bottom left and the page number 31 at bottom right, all at about 94% from the top.

**Visuals**

Left: a schematic chart with no axes, values or legend, titled CAI sales growth and captioned Schematic illustration only. A lime-green (#D6FE79) curve rises from the lower left at about 58% height, flattens slightly, then climbs steeply to the upper right, filled below with a gradient from lime to teal to blue; a lime arrowhead sits at its top right. A white straight arrow runs from the lower left to the right at about 45% height with a white arrowhead. Background of the panel is a vertical gradient from deep blue #0064B4 at the top to white at the bottom left. Thin lime and white connector lines run horizontally from the chart to the right-hand boxes, each ending in a filled circle (lime line to the upper box at about 38% height, white line to the lower box at about 65% height). Upper right box, three photographs side by side, unboxed and butted together: (1) a close-up render of red and blue spheres joined by stick bonds like a molecule model on a pale blue-white background with blurred red and blue specks; (2) orange and blue flame or smoke swirls on a black background; (3) a row of clear plastic water bottles with blue caps, in receding perspective on white. Lower right box, three photographs: (1) a Paris street scene with the Eiffel Tower seen through an ornate Haussmann-style building, warm beige and green tones, with a grey 50% black tint over the photo; (2) hands holding a fan of colour swatches over a red surface, with a dark 50% black tint; (3) an aerial night view of a port with cranes and lit container ship, overlaid with a blue network graphic of connected nodes and icons (cloud, wifi symbol, lock, device) in cyan, with a dark 50% black tint at the bottom. Logo: the Avient wordmark with a diamond-lattice symbol in white at bottom left. Photos are square-cornered rectangles, not cropped into shapes, with no rounded corners or shadows.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF.

### Slide 31

**Text**

Intersecting secular trends and high growth markets with / our technologies to create product platforms of scale / Housing shortage / Increased power generation and distribution needs / driven by high performance computing and electric mobility / Heightened geopolitical tensions / Move to sustainability solutions / Tightening regulatory environment / Personalized, self-administered / healthcare solutions / Composites for building and construction / Composites for electrification and energy / CAI PORTFOLIO / Flame retardants / Composites for Defense and law enforcement / Non-PFAS functional additives / Plastic lightweighting / Engineered materials for healthcare / Drug delivery devices / Copyright © 2024 . / Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved / 32

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a full-bleed blue slide. The title sits top-left across two lines at about 3% left margin and 6% top. The left column holds six right-pointing chevron bars (x 3.1 to 47.3%) stacked from y 24.3% to 88.5%, with the text left-aligned inside each bar. The right column (x 49.5 to 97.5%) lists eight product rows separated by thin rules, with three rows boxed in lime outlines. The Avient logo sits bottom-left at y 94%, the copyright block beside it, and the page number '32' bottom-right at x 95.5%.

**Visuals**

Six right-pointing chevron arrow blocks, one per left-column label: Housing shortage; Increased power generation and distribution needs; Heightened geopolitical tensions; Move to sustainability solutions; Tightening regulatory environment; Personalized, self-administered healthcare solutions. Each chevron has a flat left edge and a point on the right, filled lime #D7FF7A and seen through a 60 percent #0095FF overlay so it appears teal #56BFCA. Thin horizontal rules on the right column separate product rows. Three lime outlined rounded boxes (#D7FF7A stroke) group right-column items: Flame retardants (with CAI PORTFOLIO tag), Non-PFAS functional additives and Plastic lightweighting, and Drug delivery devices. A lime pill tag labelled CAI PORTFOLIO with a small downward pointer sits above the Flame retardants box. The Avient logo (white wordmark with a diamond-lattice mark) sits bottom-left. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background: Solid colour #0095FF (bright blue). Solid bright blue background #0095FF. Title in bold sans-serif (Arial Bold) at about 5.9 percent of slide height, rendered light blue #66BFFF because of the 60 percent blue overlay over the top band. Left labels in Arial Bold about 3.0 percent of slide height, rendered dark blue #00599A through the overlay (recorded colour #000000). Right-column text in Arial Bold about 3.0 percent; highlighted items are pure white #FFFFFF while non-highlighted items are faded #4CB4FF under a 70 percent blue overlay. Accent colour lime #D7FF7A for outlines, chevrons and the CAI tag (#D5FF79). Chevrons are flat-filled with no shadows or gradients. Outline boxes have small corner radii (about 1 percent of slide width) and stroke width about 0.5 percent of slide height. Separator rules are thin light blue lines. Footer text in Arial regular about 1.5 percent of slide height, white.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

### Slide 32

**Review notes**

- Review: The blue and lime identity is consistent, section dividers (slides 1, 26, 39, 46, 54) and the tags 'Catalyze the core' and 'Build new platforms of scale' help orientation, and the slide 32 and 44 highlight pages tie each market page back to the trends.

**Text**

CATALYZE THE CORE
Drug delivery devices
$6B ADDRESSABLE MARKET
MARKET DRIVERS & NEEDS
• Regulatory compliance
• Reliable material quality and uninterrupted supply
• Supply chain risk mitigation
• Global reach
AVIENT VALUE PROPOSITION
• ISO 13485 accredited sites
• Globally approved raw material portfolio
• Strict change management
• Global supply
AVIENT END MARKETS, APPLICATIONS
Auto injectors
Syringes
Inhalers
OTHER APPLICATONS
• In-vitro diagnostics
• Remote monitoring
• Pharma packaging
• Catheters
• Medical equipment
Copyright © 2024 .
Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved
33

**Layout**

Full-bleed layout with a solid blue header band across the top and a blue footer band along the bottom. A photograph fills the left side of the body, and the right side holds two text columns: Market Drivers & Needs on the left and Avient Value Proposition on the right, both starting below a lime banner. The title sits top-left inside a white tag, with the page number bottom-right and the logo and copyright bottom-left.

**Visuals**

Photograph of four drug delivery pens (auto-injector style) lying diagonally from lower left toward upper right, with green caps, dark grey-purple bodies and one lavender-capped pen; blurred teal fabric beneath and a blurred pale background at top left. Soft natural daylight from the upper left, cool teal and grey palette, cropped at the slide edge and full-height in the left 55 percent, fading to white at its right edge. Three circular photos in the teal band: a woman in a white top holding a white pen-style injector against her upper arm (labelled Auto injectors); two glass syringes with blue-tinted needle hubs on a dark background (labelled Syringes); a blue inhaler and a white canister on a light grey background (labelled Inhalers). Each circle is about 9 percent of slide width, aligned on one row. No charts or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF, with a white panel and photograph in the body area. Solid bright blue #0095FF background with a white header tag, lime #D5FF79 banner and thin lime rule, and a translucent teal #00A8AA band behind the applications row. Title text is white #FFFFFF bold sans-serif (Arial Bold), about 5.9 percent of slide height. Column headings are black #000000 bold sans-serif in letterspaced capitals about 1.9 percent of slide height. Body bullets are black #000000 regular Arial about 2.6 percent of slide height. The applications heading is teal #00A8AA bold capitals. Application labels and list are white #FFFFFF Arial about 2.2 percent. Bullets are plain round dots. Shapes are square-cornered with no visible shadows, except a soft drop shadow under the white tag. Margins are about 3 percent on the left and 2 percent on the right.

**Text**

CATALYZE THE CORE / Plastic lightweighting / $5B ADDRESSABLE MARKET / MARKET DRIVERS & NEEDS / • Reducing material and energy usage / • Achieving sustainability goals and objectives / • Improved product performance, structural integrity / AVIENT VALUE PROPOSITION / • Best-in-class additives for a wide range of polymer processing temperatures / • Design services to simulate foaming and mold designs / AVIENT END MARKETS, APPLICATIONS / Building and construction / Wood plastic composite decking / Transportation / Bottles and food packaging / Copyright © 2024 . / Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved / 34

**Layout**

Full-bleed slide with a saturated blue #0095FF frame: a title band across the top (label box and title at left), a left photo column spanning roughly the left 60 percent of the body, and a two-column text area at right (Market Drivers & Needs at left, Avient Value Proposition at right) under a lime market banner. A translucent purple strip runs across the lower right holding four circular application images with captions; a footer band at the bottom carries the Avient logo, copyright lines at left and page number 34 at right. Left margin about 3 percent; the title sits top-left, below the CATALYZE THE CORE label; text is left-aligned.

**Visuals**

Left: a photograph of soap bubbles floating in front of a blurred green forest canopy in warm sunlight, with iridescent pink, green, blue and purple bubble films of varying sizes, the largest in the lower left and a cluster at centre-left; composition is landscape, cropped to the left column with a white gradient fade on its right edge, and the bubbles sit over a soft lime-green and white background. Lower right: a purple translucent panel (#7030A0 at 30 percent, #7E5E96 at 30 percent and #5C3A76 at 40 percent layered with diagonal edges) behind four circular photographs, each about 9 percent of slide width and 16 percent of height, with captions beneath: (1) a modern glass and steel office and residential building complex at dusk with lit windows; (2) stacked wood-plastic composite decking boards with metal fasteners on a pallet-like arrangement, warm brown tones; (3) a car interior dashboard with white steering wheel, orange-accented trim and a central touchscreen, cool grey tones; (4) a person in a white sleeve arranging plastic water bottles and food packages in a fridge, white and blue tones. Each circle is a full circular crop with no border. Logo: Avient wordmark with a stylised white geometric mark at bottom left.

**Design**

Background bright blue #0095FF. Title 'Plastic lightweighting' and label text in white #FFFFFF, title Arial Bold at about 5.9 percent of slide height; the CATALYZE THE CORE label is blue #0095FF Arial Bold capitals with wide letter-spacing at about 2.6 percent, inside a white box with soft drop shadow. Body text black #000000 Arial regular at about 2.6 percent of slide height; section headings black #000000 Arial Bold capitals with wide letter-spacing at about 1.9 percent; the end-markets heading is dark purple #50164A. Accent lime #D5FF79 for the market banner and a thin top rule. Purple translucent panels #7030A0, #7E5E96, #5C3A76. Captions white Arial regular at about 2.2 percent. Shapes are square-cornered with no visible shadows apart from the label box; the banner has a slanted left edge.

### Slide 34

**Text**

BUILD NEW PLATFORMS OF SCALE
Non-PFAS functional additives
$8B ADDRESSABLE MARKET
MARKET DRIVERS & NEEDS
• Regulatory restrictions with the removal of PFAS in multiple end markets
• Major retailers requiring PFAS content declaration from suppliers
• Enhanced plastic properties, aesthetics and processability
• Specialized functionalities making plastics a safe option in demanding applications
AVIENT END MARKETS, APPLICATIONS
Flexible film
Artificial turf
Food storage, oil containers
OTHER APPLICATONS
• Electrical connectors
• Labware
• Food contact appliances
Copyright © 2024 .
Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved
35

**Layout**

Full-bleed slide with a bright blue header band across the top holding a lime eyebrow label and the title, a lime rule beneath it, and a two-part body: a blurred molecule photograph fills the left 55% and a white text column fills the right side. The top-right area holds a lime banner stripe with the market figure; a translucent blue-violet band with three circular photos and a text column for other applications runs along the lower right, and a blue footer band holds the logo, copyright lines and slide number. Left margin is about 3% of slide width for the title and about 47% for the right column text, which is left aligned.

**Visuals**

Photograph of a molecular model rendered as glossy spheres joined by translucent tubes. A large blue sphere at centre-left is bonded to three red spheres (one upper-left, one right, one bottom). A second, smaller molecule sits at centre-right with a blue sphere in the middle, a red sphere at upper-left and a red sphere at bottom, linked by pale tubes. The background is a soft white-to-light-blue gradient with out-of-focus red and blue bokeh dots. Lighting is studio-style with specular highlights on each sphere. The photo is not cropped and fills the left panel edge to edge with a white fade on its right edge. Three circular photographs sit in a row: (1) flexible film: stacks of clear stretch-wrap rolls on cores with a green plastic fitting, white and grey tones; (2) artificial turf: a white golf ball on green turf with a yellow flag pole against a blurred blue sky and trees; (3) food storage and oil containers: golden oil pouring from a yellow-gold container over a white marble-like surface. Each circle is about 9% of slide width and 16% of slide height, cropped to a circle, with a light blue #85BADD disc behind it. The 'AVIENT' logo is in white at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid bright blue #0095FF background with the molecule photograph on the left and a white panel on the right.

**Layout**

Full-bleed slide with a solid blue header band across the top holding the title and subtitle at left, and a solid blue footer band along the bottom holding the logo and copyright at left and the page number at right. Between the bands a full-width three-column panel runs edge to edge: a photograph at the left (about 0 to 50 percent of width), then a white EXISTING column, a green EVOLVING arc band in the middle and a beige EMERGING column at the right; the category labels sit centred at the top of their columns, with the item circles stacked in the middle and right columns and the pipette tip circle in the left column.

**Visuals**

Photograph at left (x 0 to about 30 percent, full height of the panel, fading to white at its right edge): a tall transparent plastic-sheathed cylindrical machine with a stainless steel shaft and a perforated grey-metal drum base, a red ring at the bottom; lighting is bright and diffuse, dominant colours are silver, grey and red. Four circular photographs and one more: about 10.5 percent width each. Circle 1 (Existing, x 28.5 y 45.9): pipette tips, a row of blue-and-red-tipped pipettes in a white rack on a blue background. Circle 2 (Evolving, x 50.2 y 30.6): polymer processing aids, silver rolls of blue-tinted film on machinery. Circle 3 (Evolving, x 50.4 y 59.6): flame retardants, circuit breakers in front of orange flames. Circle 4 (Emerging, x 76.1 y 30.6): medical tubing, gloved hands in blue scrubs holding a green syringe-tipped tube. Circle 5 (Emerging, x 76.1 y 59.5): solvent barrier, gold oil pouring from a bottle against a grey-white crumpled background. Green curved band with a lighter lime right edge is a gradient shape, not a photo. Avient logo at bottom left.

**Design**

Background is blue #0095FF for header and footer bands; central panel background is white #FFFFFF at left, green gradient from #00B050 to lime #D4FF6B forming a curved arc in the middle, and beige #ECE2D8 at right. Title is white #FFFFFF Arial Bold, approximately 5.4 percent of slide height. Subtitle is white #FFFFFF Arial regular, about 3.2 percent of slide height. Category labels EXISTING and EMERGING are bold grey #7C7873 in widely spaced capitals about 2.6 percent; EVOLVING is bold white #FFFFFF in the same size. Item headings are bold black #000000 Arial about 2.0 percent of slide height; item descriptions are regular black #000000 Arial about 1.7 percent, with the pipette tip and medical tubing descriptions in grey. Page number and footer copyright lines are white Arial about 1.5 to 2.2 percent. Circles are photographs with circular crops and a thin light grey edge. No corner radii; the arc has a soft dark shadow along its edge.

### Slide 36

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A simple three-driver margin bridge with numbered callouts shows how expansion is decomposed into operating leverage, mix and productivity.
- Review: Weaknesses are material: slide 3 is an unreadable wall of legal text, several slides carry very small text (the captions on slides 12 and 13, the labels on slides 18, 36 and 43, the sources on slide 51), and some layouts are partly empty or washed out, such as the large blue gap on slide 13 and the faded text on slides 58 and 11.
- Worth keeping: Keep the repeated segment template, the numbered margin bridges with lime driver tables (slides 36, 52, 59), the 'existing, evolving, emerging' flow diagrams, and the consistent colour coding and tag labels.
- Suggested repair: Cut the legal text on slide 3 to a readable notice or move it to the appendix, enlarge the captions and diagram labels on slides 12, 13, 18, 36, 43 and 51, and enlarge or reflow the reconciliation table on slide 64.
- What would change the score: I would reduce the stock-photo decoration and the repeated strategic-drivers pages (15, 38, 53) so more space goes to larger charts and the key numbers, and label the values in the bridge on slides 36 and 52 as slide 59 does.

**Text**

Margin expansion opportunity
CAI margin expansion / Schematic illustration only
+350bps / margin expansion
Operating / leverage
• Organic volume growth and / SG&A efficiencies from / prioritizing resources
1
Mix / improvement
• Increased sales in higher margin / growth platforms such as / functional additives

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a full-bleed blue field: a white schematic panel on the left (about 3% to 49% of width) and a three-row table of green label cells and white text cells on the right (about 51% to 96%). The title sits top-left at about 6% from the top and 3% from the left edge, with the panel tops at 16% and bottoms at about 91%. Footer logo and copyright text sit bottom-left, and the page number 37 sits bottom-right; the margins are roughly 3% on the sides.

**Visuals**

Schematic illustration of a margin bridge (not a chart with axes). Two solid vertical bars: a short blue bar at the left (bottom-aligned, top at about 64.8% of slide height) and a tall blue bar at the right (top at about 30.2%), both sitting on a shared baseline near 94%. Three upward green block arrows step up between them from left to right: Productivity (tallest step lowest, top at about 50.6%), Mix improvement (top at about 40.6%), and Operating leverage (top at about 30.2%, reaching the right bar's top). Each arrow has a light green (#D5FF79) head fading to mid green (#6CC05E) at the shaft, with a white fade at the shaft base. A thin blue (#0095FF) horizontal line at about 30.3% runs from a small solid blue dot at about x 15.6% across to the top of the right bar, labelled +350bps margin expansion. A soft grey-white gradient shadow strip lies along the baseline under the panel. Lighting is flat; no photographs. Right side: three rows, each with a dark olive (#2A3320) numbered circle (1, 2, 3) in light green text, a bold black heading, and bullet points. Thin blue (#0095FF) horizontal rules separate the rows. The AVIENT wordmark logo sits bottom-left in white.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF covering the full slide. Solid saturated blue field with white headline and white footer text. Left panel and right text cells are white; the label column is pale lime green. Title in Arial Bold, white, about 32pt (5.9% of slide height), with no letter-spacing. Panel label 'CAI margin expansion' in Arial Bold, blue #0095FF, about 11pt; subtitle 'Schematic illustration only' in Arial Regular, blue #0095FF, about 10pt. '+350bps' in Arial Bold, black, about 24pt. Heading words in Arial Bold, black, about 18pt; bullet body text in Arial Regular, black, about 16pt. Numbered circles and numerals in light green #D5FF79 Arial Bold. Footer in Arial Regular, white, about 8pt; page number Arial Regular, white, about 12pt. Shapes have square corners, no visible shadows except the baseline gradient strip; panel and cells have no borders. Gradients appear on the bars (blue #0095FF at top to dark blue #00508F at bottom) and on arrows (light green to mid green with white fade).

### Slide 37

**Text**

Color, Additives and Inks – strategic drivers
What are we doing differently ?
Portfolio
prioritization
Amplify
innovation
Digital
for operational excellence
and growth
Leadership, talent
and culture
for the Avient of the future
1
Prioritizing and expediting development and commercialization of high-growth functional additives
2
Driving customized growth tactics worldwide for each region
3
Leverage digital tools to increase operation efficiencies and enhancing customer interactions
4
Streamlined organization with fewer business units to reduce internal complexity and better serve customers
Copyright © 2024 .
Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved
38

**Layout**

Title is top-left at about 3% margin, with a one-line subtitle beneath it. A row of four equal-width photo tiles (each about 24% wide, 40% tall, gutters about 1.6%) sits across the middle, with a white panel below holding four numbered lines. The footer has the Avient logo at bottom left with copyright text beside it and the page number at bottom right; all text is left-aligned except the page number, which is right-aligned.

**Visuals**

Four photographs in a horizontal row, each a square-cornered tile with a dark gradient overlay at the bottom where the white captions sit. Tile 1 (left): a hand holding a gold coin above stacks of gold coins on a dark grey hexagonal tile surface; hand enters from top right; grey and gold palette; close framing; cropped at edges. Tile 2: a hand holding a glowing transparent light bulb with a luminous brain inside against a dark blue background with a network of glowing dots and lines shifting to magenta at right; centred subject, shallow depth of field. Tile 3: an aerial dusk view of a container port with cranes and a ship lit in orange and purple, overlaid with a translucent network of connected nodes and icons (cloud, X mark, wifi symbol in a glowing circle at centre-right, small app icons); purple and blue dominant with orange lights. Tile 4: close view of rowers' oars and arms in navy athletic sleeves on a boat, grey sky and light grey background, diagonal oars from upper left; cropped at right edge. Below the tiles, a soft grey drop shadow runs along the top edge of the white panel. Four blue circles with white bold numerals 1 to 4 sit at the left of the list lines. The Avient logo (diamond lattice mark with AVIENT wordmark) sits at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF covering the full slide.

**Layout**

Full-bleed blue slide with the title across the top left at about 5% from the left edge. Below it a lime panel spans the width with a bold headline on the left, a thin vertical blue divider at about 54%, and a three-column bullet list on the right. Beneath, a white panel with a blue-to-white gradient at its top edge holds three elements in a row: a navy circle at left and two donut charts at centre and right. The Avient logo sits bottom left with copyright text beside it, and the page number sits bottom right; both are on the blue background.

**Visuals**

Three visuals. (1) Navy circle #2C2E5B, centred about x 17%, y 69%, diameter about 11.6% of width, containing the text '2023 SALES' in small light grey-white sans-serif capitals above '$1.1B' in large bold white sans-serif. (2) Donut chart titled by its centre label '2023 sales / by region', no axes, no legend box; three segments with printed values: Americas 55% in orange #FF7D41 (label placed inside segment, label 'Americas' outside to the right), Europe, Middle East, Africa 32% in blue #0095FF (label outside lower right), Asia 13% in navy #2C2E5B (label 'Asia' outside lower left). The donut hole is white. Segments run clockwise from the top: orange about 55%, then blue about 32%, then navy about 13%. (3) Donut chart centred on '2023 sales / by business unit', no axes, no legend box; two segments with printed values: Engineered Materials 44% in orange #FF7D41 (label top, 'Engineered Materials' outside upper right) and Composite Materials 56% in blue #0095FF (label outside lower right). 41

**Layout**

Full-bleed slide with a title at top left, a white attribute panel across the upper middle, and a lime-green band across the lower half holding six photo cards in one row. Title left-aligned at about 3% from the left edge, with the panel and cards sharing roughly 3% side margins. Footer sits on the blue bar at the bottom: Avient logo at left, copyright lines beside it, and the page number '41' at the right margin. The 'APPLICATIONS' label sits on a tab that straddles the top edge of the green band, centred horizontally.

**Visuals**

Ten attribute icons in one row inside the white panel, each a grey (#8C8C8C-ish) rounded-square tile about 4.6% wide with a white line glyph: 1) Strength, stiffness: dumbbell; 2) Durability: hammer; 3) Purity: water drop with two sparkles; 4) Elasticity: two dashed wave lines with a down arrow and an up arrow around a horizontal bar; 5) Fire retardancy: flame inside a circle with a diagonal slash; 6) Barrier properties: three down arrows above wavy layer lines; 7) Conductivity: three up arrows above a layered block; 8) Color and UV stability: sun above a sloped line with an arrow; 9) Sustainability: recycling triangle of arrows with a leaf; 10) Processing ease: factory building with sawtooth roof and three windows. Six photographs in equal portrait cards (about 15% wide by 41% tall, sharp rectangular edges, no rounding): 1) Healthcare devices: close-up of a light-skinned upper arm with a round white adhesive sensor patch, a hand holding a smartphone showing a colourful health dashboard with circular gauges; cool daylight, white and skin tones with dark screen. 2) Semiconductor manufacturing: glowing green square microchip on a blue circuit board with light-blue traces and ring pads, bright neon glow, dark blue surround. 3) Wire and cable solutions: several loose wires (white, pink, grey, green, blue) fanning up from two cut black cable ends against a dark teal background, dramatic lighting. 4) Consumer goods: smiling child underwater in a swimming pool wearing blue goggles with a red strap, sunlit turquoise water and rippling light, full-frame. 5) Transportation: close-up of a white and grey electric-vehicle charging connector plugged into a car's charging port, with a yellow charging cable and a blue status light, blue-grey car body. 6) Telecom: lattice steel telecom tower with white panel antennas against a clear blue sky, viewed from below. Each photo has a dark translucent label bar across its lower part (about 9.3% tall) with opacities of 33%, 46%, 27%, 29%, 23% and 36% in black, over which the white bold labels sit. A thin grey shadow strip runs along the top edge of the green band.

**Design**

Background: Solid bright blue #0095FF across the whole slide, with a white panel and lime-green band layered over it.

### Slide 41

**Text**

Composite materials / KEY FORM FACTORS / Engineered fibers / Tapes, fabrics / Laminates, panels / Pultruded components / Shapes / Long-fiber thermoplastics / APPLICATIONS / Electrical / infrastructure / Ballistic / protection / Composite / lumber / Fiber optic / cables / Outdoor high / performance / Trucks, / rail, RV / AVIENT / Copyright © 2024 . / Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved / 42

**Layout**

Full-bleed bright blue slide with the title top-left at about 3% from the left edge, aligned to the left margin shared by the white panel and footer. A white rectangular panel spans nearly the full width (x 3.1 to 96.9%) from y 16.1 to 46.7%, holding the KEY FORM FACTORS heading and a row of six equal image-and-caption columns. A full-width lime band runs from y 44.4% to the bottom of the content area at 90.6%, with the APPLICATIONS tab overlapping the top edge of the band at centre, and six equal-width vertical tiles in a row inside it. The footer sits on blue below the band with the logo and copyright at left and the page number at right.

**Visuals**

Two groups of photographs. Key form factors row, left to right: (1) Engineered fibers: close-up of a spool of pale grey-green continuous fiber strands wound tightly, soft even light, cropped as a rectangle. (2) Tapes, fabrics: several large rolls of beige and tan tape with blue-grey edges stacked in rows, shallow depth of field, boxed rectangle. (3) Laminates, panels: stacked composite panels and a sheet in green, cream and dark grey on a light grey backdrop, boxed. (4) Pultruded components: three hollow black tubular profiles standing upright on a white background, boxed. (5) Shapes: two blue machined composite bars with bolt holes crossing over a grey plate on white, boxed. (6) Long-fiber thermoplastics: a dark grey coiled tube end with a pile of chopped grey-black fibres on white, boxed. Applications row, left to right: (1) Electrical infrastructure: steel lattice transmission towers and insulators silhouetted against a low sun with warm orange and blue sky, full tile. (2) Ballistic protection: a soldier in multicam camouflage with tan helmet, ear protection, sunglasses and headset, holding a rifle, against a blue sky, full tile. (3) Composite lumber: close-up of grey-brown wood-grain composite boards with routed edges and a light cream background, full tile. (4) Fiber optic cables: bundle of orange-red cable ends with fibres glowing and fanning out, binary code pattern in background, warm orange light, full tile. (5) Outdoor high performance: archer drawing a black and blue compound bow, wearing a yellow and black shirt and cap, blue sky and trees, full tile. (6) Trucks, rail, RV: front of a red and white truck cab with a chrome fuel tank and warm sunlight flare, full tile. Each applications tile carries a dark translucent band across its lower part with white bold labels. Logo: AVIENT wordmark with a white geometric diamond-lattice mark at bottom left, white.

**Design**

Background bright blue #0095FF for the top and bottom areas, with a white panel #FFFFFF for key form factors and a lime #D5FF79 band for applications. Title is white #FFFFFF bold Arial-style sans-serif at about 5.9% of slide height, body labels are black #000000 regular Arial at about 2.0%, tile labels are white #FFFFFF bold Arial at about 3.0%, footer text is white regular Arial at about 1.5%. The KEY FORM FACTORS heading is letter-spaced bright blue #3DA8F5 bold caps at about 2.2%. The APPLICATIONS tab text is dark olive grey #3B4A2A bold caps, letter-spaced. Shapes are rectangles with square corners, no outline. The white panel has a soft grey drop shadow at its bottom edge. Each applications tile has a black overlay at 23 to 46% opacity over its lower band. Tile photos have square corners and thin shadows.

### Slide 42

**Text**

Technology hybridization
Combining multiple material technology platforms to deliver unique solutions
EXISTING
EVOLVING
EMERGING
Marine / composite / panels
Building & / construction / composite panels
Fire-retardant / composite / panels
Next gen fire-retardant / UV resistant materials
Fire-retardant, / UV resistant / composite utility poles
UV resistance
Fire retardants
Insulators
Composite pultruded rods
Composite / utility poles
43
Copyright © 2024 .
Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved

**Layout**

Full-bleed slide with a blue header band holding the title and subtitle at top left, and a blue footer band at the bottom. Between the bands, a three-zone horizontal flow runs left to right: EXISTING on a white zone (left, about 0 to 45% width), EVOLVING on a green gradient zone with a curved left edge (about 37 to 80% width), and EMERGING on a beige zone (right, about 80 to 100%). Column labels sit at the top of each zone at about 21% height. Circular photos sit on three rows with arrows between them; the title is left-aligned at about 3% margin. Footer logo at left, copyright text beside it, page number 43 at bottom right; no slide-number placeholder beyond that.

**Visuals**

Diagram of technology hybridization with 13 circular photographs, arrows and text labels. Left column (white zone): row 1 circle of an ivory marine composite panel stack on a base (labelled Marine composite panels) with a green gradient arrow pointing right to a circle of building composite panels; row 2 circle of a purple-orange sunset over sea (labelled UV resistance) with a faded green arrow running right to a circle of orange flames (labelled Fire retardants); row 3 circle of a white silicone insulator on a composite pultruded rod (labelled Insulators, Composite pultruded rods) with a green arrow pointing right into the green zone. Middle column (green zone): building composite panels circle (ivory and grey panel stack on a base) with a dark green arrow pointing right to a circle of a grey-white construction wall panel under a crane against blue sky labelled Fire-retardant composite panels; a central circle of flames in orange and blue on black labelled Next gen fire-retardant / UV resistant materials, connected by curved dark green arrows up to the Fire-retardant composite panels arrow and down to a long dark green arrow pointing right; a circle of grey stacked composite tubes labelled Composite utility poles with a dark green arrow pointing right. Right column (beige zone): circle of a utility pole and cross-arms with wires against sky labelled Fire-retardant, UV resistant composite utility poles. Photographs are circular crops with thin light grey rims and soft shadows, not boxed. Arrows are dark green (#0B7A3A to #00A550) gradient shafts with solid triangular heads.

**Design**

Background: Solid #FFFFFF base, with the header and footer bands in #0095FF and a three-zone band between them: white #FFFFFF, green gradient #00B050 to #D4FF6B, and beige #ECE2D8. Clean corporate look. Header and footer bands are solid bright blue #0095FF. Title is bold white sans-serif (Arial Bold style) about 5.9% of slide height, left-aligned. Subtitle is regular-weight white sans-serif (Arial style) at about 3.5% of slide height. Zone labels are bold sans-serif in uppercase: EXISTING and EMERGING in warm grey #7C7873, EVOLVING in white. Element labels are bold Arial-style sans-serif about 2.0% of slide height: grey #545859 on the white zone, white on the green zone for Building & construction composite panels, black #000000 elsewhere. Footer copyright in regular Arial-style white about 1.5% of height; page number in regular white about 2.2%. Green zone gradient runs from #00B050 on the left to light lime #D4FF6B on the right edge. Beige zone #ECE2D8. Shapes have hard curved edges, no rounded corners on bands, no shadows other than a faint soft shadow along the green zone curve.

### Slide 43

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are material: slide 3 is an unreadable wall of legal text, several slides carry very small text (the captions on slides 12 and 13, the labels on slides 18, 36 and 43, the sources on slide 51), and some layouts are partly empty or washed out, such as the large blue gap on slide 13 and the faded text on slides 58 and 11.
- Suggested repair: Cut the legal text on slide 3 to a readable notice or move it to the appendix, enlarge the captions and diagram labels on slides 12, 13, 18, 36, 43 and 51, and enlarge or reflow the reconciliation table on slide 64.

**Text**

Intersecting secular trends and high growth markets with / our technologies to create product platforms of scale / Housing shortage / Increased power generation and distribution needs / driven by high performance computing and electric mobility / Heightened geopolitical tensions / Move to sustainability solutions / Tightening regulatory environment / Personalized, self-administered / healthcare solutions / SEM PORTFOLIO / Composites for building and construction / Composites for electrification and energy / Flame retardants / Composites for Defense and law enforcement / Non-PFAS functional additives / Plastic lightweighting / Engineered materials for healthcare / Drug delivery devices / Copyright © 2024 . / Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved / 44

**Layout**

Full-bleed slide with the title across the top in two lines, left-aligned at about 3% margin. Below, a left column of six stacked right-pointing block arrows runs from about 24% to 88% of slide height, and a right column of text rows sits in outlined rounded groups with the SEM PORTFOLIO pill on the top edge of the first group. The Avient logo sits bottom-left with the copyright text beside it and the page number at bottom-right, about 96% from the left.

**Visuals**

Diagram with no connecting arrows: six teal block arrows (rectangles with pointed right ends, tip about 47% across the slide) stacked vertically in the left column, labelled Housing shortage, Increased power generation and distribution needs (with a subtitle line driven by high performance computing and electric mobility), Heightened geopolitical tensions, Move to sustainability solutions, Tightening regulatory environment, and Personalized, self-administered healthcare solutions. In the right column, a lime-outlined rounded box (SEM PORTFOLIO group) contains four white rows separated by thin white rules: Composites for building and construction; Composites for electrification and energy; Flame retardants; Composites for Defense and law enforcement. Below it, two rows are dimmed by a 70% blue overlay: Non-PFAS functional additives and Plastic lightweighting. A second lime-outlined rounded box contains Engineered materials for healthcare and Drug delivery devices, separated by a thin white rule. The SEM PORTFOLIO label is a lime pill with a small downward tail pointing at the first group. Logo: the Avient wordmark with its diamond-lattice mark in white, bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Solid #0095FF bright blue, full slide. Solid saturated bright blue background #0095FF. Title is Arial Bold, white #FFFFFF under a 60% #0095FF overlay so it reads light blue (about #66BFFF); size about 5.9% of slide height, two lines, tight leading. Left-column labels are Arial Bold, black #000000 under a 60% #0095FF overlay so they read dark blue, about 3.0% of slide height. Right-column labels are Arial Bold white #FFFFFF, about 3.0% of slide height; the two dimmed rows are under a 70% overlay. Accent colour is lime #D7FF7A, used for the arrow fills (seen as teal #56BFCA after the overlay), the outline boxes, and the SEM PORTFOLIO pill. Thin white horizontal rules at about 0.1% height. Arrows and boxes have square-cornered arrows and rounded outline boxes with radius about 1.5% of slide height; no shadows or gradients. Footer text is Arial regular, white, about 1.5% of slide height.

### Slide 44

**Review notes**

- Review: The blue and lime identity is consistent, section dividers (slides 1, 26, 39, 46, 54) and the tags 'Catalyze the core' and 'Build new platforms of scale' help orientation, and the slide 32 and 44 highlight pages tie each market page back to the trends.

**Text**

CATALYZE THE CORE
Engineered materials for healthcare
$6B ADDRESSABLE MARKET
• Global adoption of remote patient continuous glucose monitoring, respiratory remedies, and biopharmaceutical processes
• Customers seek global suppliers, with required quality systems, that offer
– Barrier properties to manage chemical ingress
– Elasticity for conformance, patient comfort and sealing
– Purity to manage extractables
– Color / UV stability to differentiate products and extend life
AVIENT TECHNOLOGIES, SOLUTIONS
Continuous glucose monitors
Respiratory care
Biopharma tubing
Catheters
Copyright © 2024 .
Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved
45

**Layout**

Full-bleed blue slide with a white eyebrow tab at top left above a large white title that sits left-aligned at about x 3 and y 8. A lime band runs under the title across the full width; the photograph fills the left half from y 17 to y 91, and a text column starts at about x 47 on the right, with a teal band of four equal circular picture-and-caption units across the lower right. A footer strip at the bottom holds the Avient logo at left, the copyright lines beside it, and the page number 45 at right.

**Visuals**

Photograph (boxed, not full-bleed, cropped at left and bottom edges): a young woman with long dark wavy hair and an orange short-sleeved top, smiling and looking down, sitting on a teal sofa in a warm, softly lit living room; she holds a grey-white applicator against her upper left arm and a small white round glucose sensor patch is on the skin. Lighting is soft natural window light; dominant colours are orange, teal, cream and white. Four circular photographs, each about 9% wide, sit on light-blue #85BADD circular discs: (1) a woman's arm with a sensor patch while a hand holds a phone showing a glucose app, captioned Continuous glucose monitors; (2) a blonde woman holding a toddler wearing an oxygen mask, captioned Respiratory care; (3) gloved hands working with clear tubing and a bioprocess vessel with orange and white components, captioned Biopharma tubing; (4) gloved hands inserting a green-tipped catheter on a blue drape, captioned Catheters. Behind the circles, translucent teal parallelogram panels in #00A8AA at 40 to 50 percent opacity form diagonal bands that fade from left to right. Avient logo (white wordmark with a white geometric chevron mark) bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF covering the whole slide, with a white field behind the text column. Solid bright blue background #0095FF with a white eyebrow tab (#FFFFFF, subtle grey drop shadow) and a lime green band #D5FF79 under the title and across the top of the text column. Eyebrow text CATALYZE THE CORE is bold blue #0095FF in an Arial-style sans-serif, all capitals with wide letter-spacing, about 2.6% of slide height. Title is bold white Arial-style sans-serif, about 5.9% of slide height. The $6B figure is bold black Arial and ADDRESSABLE MARKET is regular black with wide tracking, both about 2.6%. Body bullets are black regular Arial, about 2.6%, with a round bullet at level one and en-dash sub-bullets (Calibri dash glyph) at level two; line spacing is generous. The section label AVIENT TECHNOLOGIES, SOLUTIONS is bold teal #00A8AA, all capitals, tracked, about 1.9%. Captions are white regular Arial, about 2.2%. Footer text is white regular Arial, about 1.5%. Shapes are rectangular with no rounded corners; the tab has a soft drop shadow.

### Slide 45

**Text**

CATALYZE THE CORE / Composites for Defense and law enforcement / $5B ADDRESSABLE MARKET / • Increase in NATO countries and defense spending / • High demand for premium performance in military and law enforcement / – reducing the weight burden / • Strong demand for high performance helmets with capability to protect / from increased threats / • Dyneema™ provides the highest performance products in the market / – up to 20% weight savings compared to competitive products / AVIENT TECHNOLOGIES, SOLUTIONS / Vests and inserts / Helmets / Shields / Vehicle armor / Copyright © 2024 . / Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved / 46

**Layout**

Full-bleed blue slide split into a left photo column (about 0 to 56% of width) and a right text column starting at about 46.9% of width. The section tag sits in a white box at top left above a title that spans the top band at x 3.1%, y 7.7%. A lime strip runs full width at y 16.1% and a lime band with the $5B heading sits at the top of the right column. Bullets stack in the right column, a small olive heading sits below them, and a row of four circular images with captions runs along the bottom right over a translucent green diagonal panel. The footer is a blue band at the bottom with the Avient logo and copyright at left and the page number at right, about x 95.5%, y 94.4%.

**Visuals**

Photograph of a soldier in multicam camouflage uniform, tan helmet with earcup and mounted sunglasses, a headset microphone, and an antenna on the back, holding a black assault rifle across the body, facing right and slightly away from the camera. The background is a clear blue sky with a white cloud at lower left. Lighting is bright daylight with warm highlights on the uniform. The photo is cropped at the bottom edge and left edge, and it is not boxed. Dominant colours are blue, tan, olive and camo green. A pale blue-to-white gradient panel sits behind the bullets on the right. Four circular photographs in a row, each about 9% wide and 16% tall: (1) a camouflage ballistic vest with a green and tan camo pattern and straps, on a white background, labelled Vests and inserts; (2) a tan and olive helmet with a mounted night-vision or optic device and a dark lens, labelled Helmets; (3) a figure in a dark grey uniform and gloves holding a black ballistic shield with a camera-like device on top, labelled Shields; (4) a tan armoured military vehicle with a dark windscreen on a green wooded background, labelled Vehicle armor. The circles have a thin light grey rim and a soft drop shadow. Translucent diagonal panels in #FFC000 at 40% opacity, #488630 at 40% and #308680 at 40% form a green-olive band behind the circles. A white AVIENT logo with stylised X mark sits in the footer at left.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF with a full-bleed photograph and gradient panels layered over it. Solid blue #0095FF background with a lime #D5FF79 strip and band as accents. Title is white #FFFFFF Arial Bold, about 5.9% of slide height, sentence case. The section tag is blue #0095FF Arial Bold capitals with wide letter-spacing, about 2.6% of height, on a white box with a soft shadow. The $5B figure is black Arial Bold and ADDRESSABLE MARKET is black Arial regular capitals with letter-spacing, both about 2.6% of height. Bullet body text is black #000000 Arial regular about 2.6% of height, with the last line in #212121. The olive heading is #686600 Arial Bold capitals with wide letter-spacing, about 1.9% of height. Captions are white Arial regular about 2.2% of height. Footer copyright is white Arial about 1.5% of height. Shapes are square-cornered with no visible border. Circles are fully round. Translucent green panels use 40% opacity.

### Slide 46

**Review notes**

- Review: The blue and lime identity is consistent, section dividers (slides 1, 26, 39, 46, 54) and the tags 'Catalyze the core' and 'Build new platforms of scale' help orientation, and the slide 32 and 44 highlight pages tie each market page back to the trends.

**Text**

BUILDING NEW / PLATFORMS OF SCALE IN... / Advanced / Composites / Mike Mosley / AVIENT INVESTOR DAY / December 4, 2024

**Layout**

Two-zone title slide: a full-bleed photograph fills the slide, and a solid blue panel occupies the right third (from about 65% to 96% of the width, full height), leaving a dark strip at the far right edge. Text is left-aligned in the panel at about x 66.7% with a left margin of about 1.7% of the width. The title block sits at the top of the panel, the portrait and green name label sit in the lower-middle, and the event name and date sit at the bottom. There is no footer or slide number.

**Visuals**

Photograph, full-bleed, filling the left part of the slide behind the blue panel: a close-up of a hand with the index finger extended from the lower left toward a bright glowing point at centre-left. Around the glow are concentric elliptical trails of light in white, blue, violet and orange, with scattered bokeh dots in blue, teal, red, yellow and purple. The surroundings are near-black navy. The lighting is a warm orange-white glow at the fingertip, which is the brightest point; the image is cropped at the left and bottom edges and is not boxed. Portrait photograph, boxed and not cropped, rectangular, at about x 66.7% y 45.3% w 16.5% h 32.0%: head-and-shoulders of a smiling middle-aged man with short greyish-brown hair, wearing a dark grey suit, white open-collar shirt and a dark tie, against a light grey studio backdrop. Framing is a straight-on medium close-up with the head in the upper half. Dominant colours are grey, dark navy and skin tones. A flat lime label bar sits directly beneath the portrait, bearing the name.

**Design**

Background: Dark navy and black photograph of light trails and bokeh, full-bleed, with a solid #0095FF blue panel over its right third.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- photo at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100: Full-bleed photograph of a hand with the index finger pointing toward a glowing orange-white light point, surrounded by concentric elliptical light trails and bokeh dots on a dark navy background; spans the whole slide and sits behind the panel (Full-bleed, uncropped by any frame; cropped by slide edges only; no overlay; the right third is covered by the panel)
- shape at x 65.2, y 0, w 30.8, h 100 [#0095FF]: Solid blue rectangle panel (Flat fill, no outline, no shadow, square corners, fully opaque; covers the right third of the photograph)
- subtitle at x 66.7, y 4.5, w 12.2, h 2.6 [Arial regular, uppercase, letter-spacing wide; font size 2.59% of slide height; #FFFFFF; left aligned]: BUILDING NEW (Two text runs on one line (BUILDING at x 66.7 w 7.5; NEW at x 74.9 w 4.6))
- subtitle at x 66.7, y 8.6, w 22.6, h 2.6 [Arial regular, uppercase, letter-spacing wide; font size 2.59% of slide height; #FFFFFF; left aligned]: PLATFORMS OF SCALE IN... (Line spacing about 4.1% of slide height between this line and the one above)

**Layout**

Two-zone layout: a full-height photograph fills the left half (about 0 to 58 percent of width) and fades into white toward the right, while a text column starts at about 47 percent of width. The title sits top-left at about 3 percent margin beneath a green label tag, with a full-width green rule under the header band. A blue footer band runs along the bottom with the logo and copyright at left and the page number at far right near the 95 percent mark.

**Visuals**

Photograph (left, about x 0 to 58 percent, y 17 to 91 percent): a construction site with a yellow tower crane lifting a light-timber prefabricated module over a multi-storey concrete frame building with red and orange scaffolding and a partly built concrete wall on the right foreground; blue sky with white clouds; daylight; dominant colours yellow, grey concrete, blue sky, light wood; the photo is full-height in its panel and fades to white at its right edge through a white gradient overlay. Second, larger photo image at x 27.7 y 17.0 w 72.2 h 73.6 is the same scene blended behind it at low opacity. Four circular photo crops in a row on the tan band at y 66.9 to 82.9 percent, each 9.0 percent wide and 16.0 percent high (diameter about 16 percent of slide height), each with a thin white ring: (1) x 48.8, a wooden staircase and deck with grey composite boards on green lawn; (2) x 61.8, white ballistic panels stacked on a black background; (3) x 74.5, a wall panel with windows against blue sky with white clouds and a crane; (4) x 87.5, a timber roof frame with rafters and braces seen from below. Each circle has a light blue #85BADD and white #FBFBFB backing shape. Corner logo: white AVIENT wordmark with diamond-grid symbol at x 2.3 y 94.1 w 7.5 h 3.2, on blue.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF behind all content, with white #FFFFFF panel on the right half. Bright blue background #0095FF with a white right-hand panel and a lime green band #D5FF79. Title text white #FFFFFF in Arial Bold, about 5.9 percent of slide height, sentence case. Label tag text in blue #0095FF Arial Bold capitals with letter-spacing, on lime #D5FF79 box with soft drop shadow. Bullet body text black #000000 Arial regular, about 2.6 percent of slide height. Section heading 'ADDRESSABLE MARKET' black Arial regular capitals with $20B in Arial Bold. Orange-brown accent #D18035 Arial Bold capitals with letter-spacing for 'AVIENT TECHNOLOGIES, SOLUTIONS'. Tan panels #D3B380, #BAA583 and #A88F83 at partial transparency form a diagonal wedge band. Captions black Arial regular about 2.2 percent of slide height. Lime #D5FF79 header band has a slanted left edge. Footer text white Arial regular about 1.5 percent. Shapes are square-cornered; no gradients beyond the photo fade.

### Slide 48

**Text**

BUILD NEW PLATFORMS OF SCALE
Composites for electrification and energy
$8B ADDRESSABLE MARKET
• Increasing electrification demands, expanding grid
• Established Avient portfolio in electrical infrastructure
• Demand driven by aging electrical infrastructure and grid hardening incentives
(e.g. 170 million wood poles over 50 years old in North America)
• Creating opportunities in all infrastructure components
• Avient differentiation is with operational velocity, manufacturing capability
and added functionality with flame retardancy, UV resistance etc.
AVIENT TECHNOLOGIES, SOLUTIONS
Utility poles
Cross arms
Insulator rods
Turbine blade stiffeners
49
Copyright © 2024 .
Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved

**Layout**

Full-bleed blue canvas with a two-column arrangement: a full-height photograph fills the left half from just below the header band to the footer, and a text column starts at about 47% of slide width with a right margin of about 2%. A lime tab with blue caps text sits top-left above a large white title spanning the top band; a thin lime rule runs full width beneath the title, and a footer strip holds the logo at lower left, copyright lines beside it and the page number 49 at lower right.

**Visuals**

Photograph on the left (about 0 to 54% width, 17 to 91% height, full-bleed to left edge) of a steel lattice electricity transmission tower seen from below, dark black-grey silhouette diagonals against a blue sky fading to orange and gold sunset clouds; a second, smaller pylon with hanging insulators recedes to the right and dissolves into a white haze gradient running from about x 30% to x 55%. Below the bullet list an orange translucent trapezoid band (about 29 to 100% width, 64 to 91% height) overlays the photo edge and the blue footer boundary, shaded in three overlapping tints of orange (#FF9D41 at 26% opacity, #FF9E2F at 76% opacity, a faint red #C00000 at 19% opacity). Four circular photographs in a row, each about 9% wide and 16% tall, cropped to circles: (1) Utility poles: a wooden utility pole with crossarm and insulators against a blue sky; (2) Cross arms: several white/grey square-section hollow composite cross arm profiles stacked in a cluster on a light background; (3) Insulator rods: two white ribbed polymer insulators with metal end fittings and two cylindrical composite insulator rods lying diagonally; (4) Turbine blade stiffeners: a white wind turbine nacelle and hub with blade roots against green and yellow farmland under a blue sky. Each circle has a thin white rim and a faint shadow. A small AVIENT logo (white wordmark with a stylised lattice mark) sits in the footer at lower left.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF across the full slide, with the photograph and orange overlays placed over it.

### Slide 49

**Text**

Specialty Engineered Materials – end markets we serve / Healthcare / Defense / Building and / construction / Energy / Packaging / Industrial / Telecom / Transportation / Consumer / Copyright © 2024 . / Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved / 50

**Layout**

Full-bleed bright blue slide with a bold white title in the top band, left-aligned at about 3% from the left edge. Below it, a light-green band holds a row of four equal photo tiles, with a white panel beneath holding a row of five photo tiles. The footer carries the Avient logo and copyright at lower left and the page number at lower right.

**Visuals**

Four photographs in the upper row, each boxed with square corners and cropped to a landscape-ish tile about 23.3% wide by 38% tall, with white bold Arial labels in the upper-left corner. Healthcare: a young woman with long dark hair and an orange T-shirt and pearl necklace, smiling and pressing a grey wearable device with a white adhesive patch onto her upper arm, seated on a teal sofa in a warm, softly lit living room; warm tones. Defense: a soldier in camouflage uniform, helmet and goggles with a headset microphone, holding a rifle, sitting in a desert with a large twin-rotor military helicopter on the ground behind him and a pale blue sky with clouds; brown and tan dominant colours. Building and construction: a crane lifting a precast concrete modular box over a multi-storey concrete building frame with orange safety railings, against a blue sky with white clouds; the label is black. Energy: high-voltage steel lattice transmission towers silhouetted against an orange-and-blue sunset, with power lines; strong backlight. Five photographs in the lower row, each about 17 to 19 percent wide by 22.7% tall, abutting with thin white gaps, each with a translucent black 30% bar along its bottom with white bold labels. Packaging: a person in a white protective suit working with a machine handling a red-tinted plastic part, purple and red lighting. Industrial: green-tinted stacks of plastic pallets or crates. Telecom: a white cellular antenna mast with multiple panel antennas against a blue sky with clouds. Transportation: a car side mirror with orange and white light streaks showing motion blur. Consumer: a bright blue flat-lay with a toothpaste tube, a razor, a white deodorant stick, sugar-like cubes and a blue cap. Also a small white Avient logo in the footer at lower left, the Avient wordmark with a stylised geometric symbol.

**Design**

Background is saturated bright blue #0095FF. Title is white Arial Bold, sans-serif, about 5.9% of slide height, not capitalised. Tile labels are white Arial Bold for most tiles and black #000000 for Building and construction, about 3.3% of slide height on upper tiles and 3.0% on lower tiles. Body and footer text is white Arial regular at about 1.5% of slide height. Accent colours: light green band #D5FF79, white panel #FFFFFF, black label bars at 30% opacity. Shapes are square cornered with no rounded corners. A soft grey shadow sits under the green band where it meets the white panel, and a thin shadow strip runs along the band's lower edge.

### Slide 50

**Text**

Our competitive advantage / Breadth and depth / of specialty solutions / Global footprint / with localized / customer intimacy / Manufacturing and / material formulation / expertise / Quality, regulatory, / and sustainability / • Engineered thermoplastics, / advanced elastomers, / additives, fibers, composites / • Unidirectional tapes, fabrics, / panels, pultrusions, shapes / • Operations in Americas, / Europe and Asia / • Source materials globally, / translate across regions / • Deep application knowledge / and design services / • Core competencies in fiber, / tape, composite lamination / and pultrusion processes / • Strong material science / expertise to address needs / • Strong regulatory and quality / management systems / • Industry accredited sites / • Recognized sustainability / ratings / Copyright © 2024 . / Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved / 51

**Layout**

Four equal vertical columns spanning the slide width, separated by thin white vertical rules at about x 26.5%, 49.9% and 73.4%. The title sits top-left at x 3.1%, y 5.7%. Each column has a centred bold heading above a stadium-shaped band of four photographs (y 21.1% to 68.2%), then left-aligned bullet text beneath. The footer holds the AVIENT logo at bottom-left and a copyright block beside it, with the page number 51 at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Four photographs in a horizontal band clipped to a stadium (pill) shape with fully rounded left and right ends. Column 1 (x 3.1%, y 32.6%, 23.5% by 24.0%): a snowboarder in a bright blue jacket, yellow pants and black helmet with goggles, riding a yellow-and-blue snowboard and throwing a plume of white powder snow against a clear blue sky; lit by bright daylight; dominant colours blue, yellow and white. Column 2 (x 26.6%, y 32.6%, 23.4% by 24.0%): several hands cupping a round green moss-covered ball in front of a blurred green background; natural daylight; dominant colours green and skin tones. Column 3 (x 50.0%, y 32.6%, 23.5% by 24.0%): a worker in a yellow hard hat, red ear defenders, safety glasses and dark work shirt typing on a laptop in front of large silver industrial pipes, ducts and a turbine; cool grey tones with yellow and red accents. Column 4 (x 73.4%, y 32.6%, 23.5% by 24.0%): a woman in a white lab coat and white gloves holding a test tube and looking at a monitor showing a blue screen, with glasses pushed up on her head in a bright laboratory; cool blue-white lighting. Overlays: blue shapes at x 0 to 100% mask the top and bottom of the band to create the stadium outline. Thin white vertical lines separate columns, from about y 16.6% to 89.3%, 0.1% wide, white. AVIENT logo (white wordmark with lattice diamond mark) at bottom-left, x 2.3%, y 94.1%, 7.5% by 3.2%.

**Design**

Background: Solid colour #0095FF (bright blue). Solid bright blue background #0095FF. Titles, headings, body text and page number are white #FFFFFF; the accent is the white rules. Typeface is Arial (Helvetica-style neo-grotesque sans-serif). Slide title is Arial Bold at about 5.9% of slide height. Column headings are Arial Bold at about 3.0% of slide height, centred. Bullet text is Arial Regular at about 2.2% of slide height, left-aligned with hanging bullets. Footer text is Arial Regular at about 1.5%. Shapes have straight white hairline rules and a stadium-shaped photo band with fully rounded ends; no shadows or gradients.

### Slide 51

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are material: slide 3 is an unreadable wall of legal text, several slides carry very small text (the captions on slides 12 and 13, the labels on slides 18, 36 and 43, the sources on slide 51), and some layouts are partly empty or washed out, such as the large blue gap on slide 13 and the faded text on slides 58 and 11.
- Suggested repair: Cut the legal text on slide 3 to a readable notice or move it to the appendix, enlarge the captions and diagram labels on slides 12, 13, 18, 36, 43 and 51, and enlarge or reflow the reconciliation table on slide 64.

**Layout**

Full-bleed blue slide with the title top-left on one line at about 5.7% from top and left margin about 3.1%. Below the title two columns: a white chart panel on the left (x 3.1 to 48.9, y 16.1 to 90.7) and a three-row white and lime table on the right (x 50.6 to 96.4, y 16.1 to 90.7). Footer runs along the bottom with the Avient logo and copyright at left and the page number at right.

**Visuals**

Schematic bar-and-arrow illustration, not data-based, titled 'SEM margin expansion' with subtitle 'Schematic illustration only'. Two blue vertical bars with a blue-to-dark-blue vertical gradient: a short bar at x 4.8, y 64.8, w 5.4, h 25.9 (left, starting point) and a tall bar at x 41.9, y 30.2, w 5.3, h 60.6 (right, end point). Three green upward block arrows with light lime top (#D5FF79) fading to green (#74C465) at bottom, stepping up from left to right: Productivity arrow (x 12.4, y 50.6, w 7.6, h 14.4), Mix improvement arrow (x 22.2, y 40.6, w 7.6, h 17.6), Operating leverage arrow (x 32.0, y 30.2, w 7.6, h 17.6); each arrow sits on a thin grey base shadow strip (x 22.2 y 53.7 w 7.5 h 4.5; x 32.0 y 43.4 w 7.6 h 4.5) and the Operating leverage arrow fades to white at its base. A thin blue horizontal reference line runs from a blue dot at about x 31.3, y 34 to the top of the tall bar at y 34, labelled '+300bps' with 'margin expansion' beneath. A grey-to-white shadow gradient strip sits along the chart baseline (x 3.1, y 88.9, w 45.8, h 1.8). Right table: three rows, each with a lime #D5FF79 left cell (x 50.6, w 15.0, h 24.9) and white right cell (x 65.6, w 30.8, h 24.9), separated by blue #0095FF horizontal rules at about y 40.9 and y 65.8. Each row has a dark olive #2A3318 filled circle with a lime #D5FF79 numeral 1, 2, 3 at its top-left. Avient logo: white wordmark with a white diamond-lattice icon, bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF. Solid saturated blue background #0095FF. Title in white #FFFFFF Arial Bold, about 5.9% of slide height, sentence case, left aligned. Chart panel label 'SEM margin expansion' in blue #0095FF Arial Bold about 2.0%; subtitle in blue #0095FF Arial regular about 1.9%. Chart labels and table headings in black #000000 Arial Bold about 3.3%; the figure '+300bps' in black Arial Bold about 4.4%; 'margin expansion' in black Arial regular about 2.2%. Table bullets in black #000000 Arial regular about 3.0% of slide height, with bullet dots. Table headings in Arial Bold 3.3%. Footer text white #FFFFFF Arial regular about 1.5%; page number white Arial regular about 2.2%. Panels are square-cornered with no shadows; the table uses flat fills and thin blue rules. Bars and arrows use vertical gradients as described.

### Slide 52

**Review notes**

- Worth keeping: Keep the repeated segment template, the numbered margin bridges with lime driver tables (slides 36, 52, 59), the 'existing, evolving, emerging' flow diagrams, and the consistent colour coding and tag labels.
- What would change the score: I would reduce the stock-photo decoration and the repeated strategic-drivers pages (15, 38, 53) so more space goes to larger charts and the key numbers, and label the values in the bridge on slides 36 and 52 as slide 59 does.
