**Layout**

Full-bleed blue slide with the title top-left at x 3.1% and a subtitle directly beneath it. A row of four equal square-cornered photo tiles spans the width from x 0.8% to 99.2% at y 19.3% to 59.3%, with a 0.8% gap between tiles. A white full-width-of-content panel sits below the tiles at y 59.4% to 90.6% holding four numbered lines. The AVIENT logo sits bottom-left at the footer, copyright text is beside it, and the page number 53 sits bottom-right at x 95.5%.

**Visuals**

Four photographic tiles in a single row, each 24.0% wide and 40.0% high, with a dark gradient overlay at the bottom where the labels sit. Tile 1 (Portfolio prioritization): a hand holding a gold coin above a stack of gold coins on a dark grey surface covered with raised hexagonal tiles outlined in gold; the hand enters from top right; neutral studio lighting; dominant colours dark grey, charcoal and gold; full-bleed inside its box, not cropped in a visible way. Tile 2 (Amplify innovation): a hand holding a clear glass light bulb with a glowing blue brain illustration inside, over a dark navy-to-magenta background with a network of glowing blue and pink dots joined by thin lines; dominant colours navy, magenta and cyan; the bulb is centred. Tile 3 (Digital for operational excellence and growth): a dusk photo of a container port with orange-lit cranes and a cargo ship, under a translucent purple and blue overlay showing a globe-like network of lines and nodes, circular white icons (a cloud, an X, a factory, a container, a house) and a larger glowing blue Wi-Fi symbol in a circle at centre right; dominant colours violet, blue and orange; the tile carries a purple tint. Tile 4 (Leadership, talent and culture): a close crop of rowers in navy kits holding black oars with lime-green grips running diagonally from upper left to lower right; grey-white sky and water; a dark gradient tints the lower part. Also a thin shadow strip along the top edge of the white panel, and a small white AVIENT wordmark logo with a diamond-lattice mark at the footer, about 7.5% wide. Four blue numbered circles (about 2.3% wide, 4.0% high, filled #0095FF) sit at the left of each bullet line; the hidden black numerals 1. to 4. at 2.22% sit under them and are not visible.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF covering the whole slide. Solid bright blue background #0095FF. Title is white (#FFFFFF) Arial Bold at about 5.9% of slide height. Subtitle is white (#FFFFFF) Arial regular, around 4.5% of height, rendered as an image. Tile labels are white (#FFFFFF) Arial Bold at 3.7% of height for the first lines, with subtitle lines in white Arial regular at 2.59%. Body bullet text is black (#000000) Arial regular at 2.96%. Numerals are white Arial Bold at 2.96% on blue #0095FF circles. Footer copyright is white Arial regular at 1.48%; page number is white Arial regular at 2.22%. Shapes are square-cornered with no visible rounding. The white panel has a faint soft shadow along its top edge. Photo tiles have no borders.

### Slide 53

**Text**

Summary / and / financials
Jamie Beggs
AVIENT INVESTOR DAY
December 4, 2024

**Layout**

Full-bleed photograph fills the left two-thirds of the slide; a solid blue panel occupies the right third (from about 65% of width to the right edge). Title sits top-left of the panel with a left margin of about 66.7% of width, stacked over three lines. A portrait photo with a name caption sits in the lower-middle of the panel, followed by an event line and date at the bottom left of the panel. No footer or slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Photograph: a human hand with an index finger extended from the lower-left, reaching toward a glowing bright orange-white point of light at the centre-left. Around the light, concentric elliptical rings of thin glowing lines in blue, violet, white and orange radiate outward, scattered with bokeh dots in blue, teal, red, yellow and purple. The hand is in shadow, lit by the glow. Composition: full-bleed, landscape, the hand cropped at the left edge and bottom-left, the light source near the horizontal centre of the photo. Lighting: dark navy/black background with warm orange-white glow. Dominant colours: deep navy #0A1A33, electric blue, orange #F5A030, white. Not boxed; no overlay on the photo. Portrait photo: a smiling woman with shoulder-length blond hair, wearing a navy blazer over a white top, against a grey studio backdrop, head and shoulders, centred, evenly lit, boxed as a rectangle with no border, sitting directly above the name caption.

**Design**

Background is a full-bleed photograph with a solid #0095FF blue panel on the right. Title text is white (#FFFFFF) Arial Bold, sans-serif, about 8.9% of slide height per line with tight leading. Name caption is black (#000000) Arial Bold on a lime fill #D7FF7A. Event line is white (#FFFFFF) Arial Bold, all caps with wide letter-spacing, about 3.0% of slide height. Date is white (#FFFFFF) Arial regular, about 3.0% of slide height. Panel and caption have square corners, no shadows or gradients.

### Slide 54

**Review notes**

- Review: The blue and lime identity is consistent, section dividers (slides 1, 26, 39, 46, 54) and the tags 'Catalyze the core' and 'Build new platforms of scale' help orientation, and the slide 32 and 44 highlight pages tie each market page back to the trends.

**Text**

The start of Avient’s next chapter...
Top line growth with / EBITDA margin expansion
Building the Avient / of the future
Incentive plans / aligned to strategy
...building upon our foundational strengths
Copyright © 2024 .
Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved
55

**Layout**

Three equal square-ish photo panels sit in a row across the middle of the slide, with a bold title across the top left and a pill-shaped banner centred below them. The title sits at the top left with a left margin of about 3 percent. The panels are spaced evenly with gaps of about 3 percent of the width and share a common top and bottom edge. The footer, with the Avient logo and copyright lines at bottom left and the page number at bottom right, sits along the bottom margin.

**Visuals**

Three photographs in a row, each boxed with a sharp-cornered rectangle and a dark translucent caption band across its lower third. Left photo: a hand with an index finger pressing a glowing hexagon icon in a network of white hexagons; the hexagons show a document with signature, a group of people, a dollar sign, a rising bar chart with arrow in a blue hexagon, a world map, gears and a magnifier over a small bar chart; blue-grey tones with a dark navy foreground, full frame, slightly blurred edges. Centre photo: a close-up of a wooden slatted wall with a 3D white and dark grey Avient logo mark and the word AVIENT in embossed letters, angled view with a pale blue sky at upper right, warm brown and tan tones. Right photo: a close-up of stacked gold coins resting in a gold scale pan hung on gold chains, warm yellow and gold tones with blurred yellow background, full frame. Each photo has a black overlay at 50 percent opacity covering its bottom 14 percent. A small Avient logo (white stacked-chevron mark plus AVIENT wordmark) sits in the footer. A lime green rounded pill banner sits below the photos.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF. Solid bright blue background #0095FF. Title text white #FFFFFF in Arial Bold, about 5.9 percent of slide height, sentence case. Caption text white #FFFFFF in Arial Bold, about 3.9 percent of slide height, left aligned. Banner text black #000000 Arial Bold about 4.4 percent of slide height. Banner fill lime #D5FF79 with fully rounded ends. Footer text white #FFFFFF in Arial regular about 1.5 percent of slide height. Page number white Arial regular about 2.2 percent. Photo frames have no border and no visible corner radius; caption bands are black at 50 percent opacity. A yellow #EFE63B panel lies behind the right photo area. Shadows are not visible.

### Slide 55

**Text**

….while enabling a sustainable world
ENABLERS AND OUTCOMES
Solutions to increase recycled content
and minimize plastic waste
Enabling customers to advance
the circular economy
RENEW
Solutions to reduce weight and
energy consumption
Shrinking carbon footprint and
decreasing emissions
REDUCE
Solutions to preserve and protect
natural resources and human life
Making our planet
a better place for all

56

**Layout**

Three full-width horizontal rows stacked in the lower two-thirds of the slide, each split into a coloured left chevron panel (about 50% width) and a light grey right panel (about 44% width), with a small all-caps centred section label above the rows. The title sits top-left at about 3% margin from the left edge, the footer runs along the bottom with the Avient logo and copyright at left and the page number at bottom right, and the rows share a left margin of about 3.1% and right edge near 96.9%.

**Visuals**

Three horizontal row graphics, each made of a coloured chevron panel on the left with a pointed right end and a light grey rectangle behind it on the right, each with a white line icon and caption. Row 1 (top): teal-blue #41BDD9 chevron with a white line icon of three looping leaves in a triangle over the label RENEW; grey right panel. Row 2 (middle): green #43B649 chevron with a white line icon of three outlined bars of decreasing height over the label REDUCE; grey right panel. Row 3 (bottom): dark teal #008383 chevron with a white line icon of a globe resting on an open hand over the label PRESERVE; grey right panel. Each icon is a flat white outline drawing with rounded line ends. No photographs or charts. The Avient logo at bottom left is a white diamond-lattice mark followed by the white wordmark AVIENT.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF covering the whole slide. Solid saturated blue background #0095FF with no gradient. The title and section label are white #FFFFFF, Arial Bold, sans-serif; the title is about 5.9% of slide height (cap height about 4.2%), and body text is about 3.3% of slide height, Arial Bold. Row left text is white #FFFFFF, right text is blue #0095FF, Arial Bold. Row chevron fills are #41BDD9, #43B649 and #008383 with a thin lighter outline; right panels are #F2F2F2. Shapes have square corners and a soft dark drop shadow beneath each row. Footer text is Arial regular, white, about 1.5% of slide height.

57

**Layout**

Full-bleed blue slide with the title top-left at about 3% left margin and 6% from top, above two stacked full-width horizontal bands. Each band has a narrow lime label column on the left (about 15% of width) and a light grey panel filling the rest, with logos laid out in a row of roughly equal columns. The footer runs along the bottom with the AVIENT logo and copyright at left and the page number 57 at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Logos and badges, no chart or photo. Top band: UN Global Compact emblem (dark navy globe with laurel wreath, no text beyond UN GLOBAL COMPACT arc lettering); Sustainable Development Goals logo (UN emblem above blue SUSTAINABLE DEVELOPMENT and multicolour GOALS wordmark with SDG wheel in the O); Responsible Care logo (navy hands holding three circles above RESPONSIBLE CARE and OUR COMMITMENT TO SUSTAINABILITY); EcoVadis gold medal (gold ring, GOLD ribbon, 2023, ecovadis Sustainability Rating on white centre); CDP Score badge (solid red #B52C34 circle with white A- and CDP Score and CDP logo). Bottom band: Morningstar Sustainalytics 'Rated' box with document icon, '17th' orange badge and PERCENTILE in Specialty Chemicals box; ISS ESG Quality Score mini table with three green rows ENVIRONMENTAL 1, SOCIAL 2, GOVERNANCE 5 (bars in green #8DC63F with white-bordered text and dark numbers at right); MSCI ESG RATINGS wordmark with orange circle A and a scale strip CCC B BB BBB A AA AAA with A highlighted orange; Barron's 100 Most Sustainable Companies 2024 square tile (navy to blue gradient with white BARRON'S serif masthead and white text); Newsweek AMERICA'S MOST RESPONSIBLE COMPANIES 2024 vertical badge (navy and gold frame, gold stars, red Newsweek ribbon, statista logo); Newsweek AMERICA'S GREENEST COMPANIES 2025 vertical badge (dark green and gold frame, red Newsweek ribbon, PLANT-A INSIGHTS GROUP logo). All logos are flat and unboxed on the grey panel, with no cropping.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF. Blue background #0095FF with white bold sans title, black bold sans uppercase band labels on lime #D5FF79 blocks, light grey #F2F2F2 panels, and white footer text. The title is Arial Bold at about 5.9% of slide height. Band label text is Arial Bold at about 2.6% of slide height, black #000000. Footer and copyright text is Arial regular about 1.5% of height, white. Page number is Arial regular about 2.2% of height, white. Lime label blocks have a faint drop shadow on the right edge. Panels have square corners with no border.

58

**Layout**

Two-column composition under a single left-aligned title at top (x 3.1%, y 5.7%). Left column is a tall gradient chart panel (x 3.1 to 26.1%, y 18.4 to 86.2%). Centre column holds two stacked boxes, a lime-green box above a white box (x 33.6 to 63.6%), each with a heading block on the left and a numbered list panel on the right (x 48.6 to 96.9%) that fades into the blue background toward the right edge. A tall lime-to-white ribbon badge sits at the right (x 77.3 to 95.1%, y 23.1 to 81.6%). Margins are about 3% on the left and 4% on the right; the AVIENT logo and copyright sit bottom-left at y 94 to 98%, and the page number 58 sits bottom-right at x 95.5%, y 94.4%.

**Visuals**

1) Left illustration, a schematic sales-growth chart in a rectangular panel with a vertical blue-to-white gradient: the upper portion is deep blue #0064A8 fading to bright blue #0095FF at its right edge, the lower portion fades from white at the left to mid blue at the right. A lime-green (#D6FE79) curved area rises from lower left to upper right, ending in a lime arrowhead pointing up-right at about x 25 to 28%, y 20 to 26%. A white arrow rises from lower left (x 4%, y 57%) to an arrowhead at about x 26%, y 43%. No axes, values or legend; the panel is captioned 'Avient sales growth / Schematic illustration only'. 2) Two thin connector lines with circular end dots: a white line at y 35.9% from about x 22.7% to the dot at x 33.5% (line fades to transparent at its left end), and a white line at y 70% from about x 8% to the dot at x 33.5%. Dots are white filled circles about 1.5% wide. 3) Badge, a filled shape with a pointed top and a notched bottom (chevron ribbon), gradient from lime #D6FE79 at top to white #FFFFFF at bottom, with a soft grey drop shadow along its edges, containing the centred text '+100 / to / +200 bps / revenue growth / above GDP'. 4) Logo: AVIENT wordmark with a diamond-lattice icon in white at bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF covers the whole slide. Solid bright blue background #0095FF with no gradient or pattern. Title in white (#FFFFFF) Arial Bold, sentence case, about 5.9% of slide height. Body and list text in white Arial regular at about 2.2%. Headings inside the boxes: 'Build new platforms of scale' in black (#000000) Arial Bold at about 3.3%; 'Catalyze the core' and 'GROW BUSINESS AT / GDP +' in bright blue (#0095FF) Arial Bold. Green box fill #D7FF7A (lime); white box fill #FFFFFF. Lime accent #D6FE79 used for the chart area, badge and arrows. Numbers 1. to 6. in white Arial at about 1.7%. Tags MARKETS, TECHNOLOGY, CUSTOMER and SCALE are small blue Arial Bold caps on lighter translucent teal-blue fills. Shapes have square corners, no borders. Thin lime and white hairline rules run along the top and bottom edges of the right-hand list panels, fading to the right. Badge has a soft drop shadow. No rounded corners.

### Slide 58

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Places the current margin, the strategic objective and the contribution of each driver side by side, giving the financial target a clear visual path.
- Review: Weaknesses are material: slide 3 is an unreadable wall of legal text, several slides carry very small text (the captions on slides 12 and 13, the labels on slides 18, 36 and 43, the sources on slide 51), and some layouts are partly empty or washed out, such as the large blue gap on slide 13 and the faded text on slides 58 and 11.

**Layout**

Full-bleed blue slide with the title across the top left at about 5.7% from the top and 3.1% from the left margin. Below, a white panel (x 3.1, y 16.1, w 45.8, h 74.6) on the left holds a schematic step chart, and a three-row table of numbered green-label cells (x 50.6 to 96.4, y 16.1 to 90.7) sits on the right, separated by blue rules. The Avient logo and copyright sit bottom left at about y 94 and the page number 59 sits bottom right at x 95.5, y 94.4.

**Visuals**

Schematic step chart (not data-labelled beyond the printed values) on a white panel. Current adjusted EBITDA margin shown as a tall blue bar labelled ~16% (bar at x 4.8, y 64.8, w 5.4, h 25.9) with caption Current adjusted EBITDA margin. Three green up-arrows stepping upward left to right: first arrow labelled +1% with caption Productivity (x 12.4, y 50.6, w 7.6, h 14.4); second arrow labelled +1% with caption Mix improvement (x 22.2, y 40.6, w 7.6, h 17.6); third arrow labelled +2% with caption Operating leverage (x 32.0, y 30.2, w 7.6, h 17.6). Each arrow has a green gradient from lighter lime at the tip to pale fade at the base and sits on a thin grey base line. A tall blue bar at x 41.9, y 30.2, w 5.3, h 60.6 labelled 20%+ with caption Strategic objective. A blue horizontal line (about 0.1% thick, #0095FF) runs from a small blue dot at about x 15.4, y 30 to the top of the 20%+ bar, beside the label +400bps margin expansion. A grey-to-white gradient floor strip sits along the bottom of the panel at x 3.1, y 88.9, w 45.8, h 1.8. Right side: three numbered rows, each with a dark olive-black filled circle containing white numerals 1, 2, 3, and bold labels Operating leverage, Mix improvement, Productivity, with bullets listed in the adjacent white cells. No photographs, logos other than the Avient wordmark in the footer.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF. Blue background #0095FF. Titles and headline numbers in white bold Arial-style sans-serif (Helvetica/Arial Bold) at about 5.9% of slide height for the title; step-panel labels in black bold Arial about 3.3%; body bullets in black regular Arial about 3.0%; small captions about 1.9% to 2.2%. Accent colour for the left panel subtitle and sublabel is blue #0095FF; numerals in the right cells are lime #D5FF79 on dark olive circles (#2B3014 approximately). Green arrows use a lime-to-green gradient (#D5FF79 to #6CC066 approximately). Bars are blue gradient #0095FF fading to dark blue #00457A at the base. Right-side label column is solid lime #D5FF79; content column white #FFFFFF; blue rules between rows. Square corners, no visible shadows except the subtle floor gradient.

### Slide 59

**Review notes**

- Worth keeping: Keep the repeated segment template, the numbered margin bridges with lime driver tables (slides 36, 52, 59), the 'existing, evolving, emerging' flow diagrams, and the consistent colour coding and tag labels.
- What would change the score: I would reduce the stock-photo decoration and the repeated strategic-drivers pages (15, 38, 53) so more space goes to larger charts and the key numbers, and label the values in the bridge on slides 36 and 52 as slide 59 does.

**Text**

Organic revenue growth with margin expansion
LONG TERM FINANCIAL TARGETS
+100 to +200bps
above GDP
Organic revenue growth
20% +
Adjusted EBITDA margin
10% +
Adjusted EPS CAGR

60

**Layout**

A single title sits top-left at about 5.7% from the top with a 3% left margin. Below it, a centred label reads LONG TERM FINANCIAL TARGETS above a wide horizontal pill-shaped band of three equal-height segments spanning roughly 2% to 98% of the width, centred vertically in the middle of the slide. The footer runs along the bottom: the AVIENT logo and a two-line copyright block at the lower left, and the page number 60 at the lower right.

**Visuals**

Three-segment horizontal pill band, left to right: segment 1 white #FFFFFF with blue text '+100 to +200bps' / 'above GDP' / 'Organic revenue growth'; segment 2 warm beige #ECE6D8 with dark text '20% +' / 'Adjusted EBITDA margin'; segment 3 lime #D5FF79 with dark text '10% +' / 'Adjusted EPS CAGR'. The outer band has fully rounded left and right ends (semicircular caps) and a soft grey inner shadow along its edges. Thin blue #0095FF divider lines separate the segments at about 34% and 66% of the width. No photographs, charts or icons apart from the logo.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF. Full-bleed saturated blue #0095FF background with no gradient or texture. Title in white #FFFFFF Arial Bold sans-serif, about 5.9% of slide height. Segment label in white #FFFFFF Arial Bold, all capitals, widely letter-spaced, about 3.3% of slide height. Large figures in Arial Bold, about 5.2% of slide height, in blue #0095FF on the white segment and dark charcoal #302E26 on the beige and lime segments. Sub-labels in Arial Bold (about 3.7%) for 'above GDP' and regular Arial (about 2.6%) for 'Organic revenue growth', 'Adjusted EBITDA margin' and 'Adjusted EPS CAGR' in matching colours. Footer text is regular Arial about 1.5% of slide height in white. Band corners fully rounded; soft shadow on the band edges; no gradients on the segments.

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a full-bleed blue field: a large left-aligned title spans the top at about 5.7% from the top, with a white square-cornered donut panel at the lower left (x 3.1 to 39.7, y 16.1 to 86.2) and a dividend bar chart on the right (x 42.9 to 95.7, y 17.1 to 86). Footer sits along the bottom with the Avient logo and copyright text at left and the page number 61 at the bottom right, all with margins of about 3 to 4 percent of the slide width.

**Visuals**

Two charts. (1) Donut chart titled Adj. Free cash flow use, subtitle 2020– 2024 est., centre label $1.1B with accumulated adj. free cash flow* 2020-2024 est. Segments clockwise from 12 o'clock: Dividend payments 37% orange #FF7D41; Share buybacks 6% navy #2C2E5B; M&A, other 27% khaki #B2A46D; Debt paydown 30% blue #0095FF. Percent labels are white bold inside each segment; category labels sit outside in their segment colour. No legend; values printed on segments; no highlighted points. (2) Vertical bar chart titled Dividend, subtitle $ per share, years 2011 to 2025 on the category axis with no numeric axis shown, gridlines at about 16, 30, 44, 58 and 72 percent of slide height, pale green #D5FF79 bars with blue #0095FF rotated value labels: 2011 $0.16, 2012 $0.20, 2013 $0.26, 2014 $0.34, 2015 $0.42, 2016 $0.50, 2017 $0.58, 2018 $0.72, 2019 $0.79, 2020 $0.82, 2021 $0.88, 2022 $0.96, 2023 $1.00, 2024 $1.03, 2025 $1.08. Bars have a faint shadow. The Avient logo is white, wordmark in bold capitals with a geometric diamond mark.

### Slide 61

**Text**

Disciplined capital allocation / 1 / Capex / Expected annual spend between 3-5% of revenue to support investment in organic growth / 2 / Dividends / Increasing each year with underlying earnings growth / 3 / Debt pay down / Target net debt to adjusted EBITDA less than 2.5x / 4 / Share repurchases / Opportunistic buy backs / 5 / M&A / De-emphasized in near term; complement organic growth strategy with M&A over time, as needed / Copyright © 2024 . / Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved / 62

**Layout**

Full-bleed blue slide with the title top-left at about 3% margin, and a single rounded-corner table of five stacked rows occupying the middle, spanning about 2.4% to 97.6% of the width. Each row has a lime left label column (about 38% wide) and a white right description column (about 57% wide), separated by a thin blue gap; a dashed white rule divides row 4 from row 5. The AVIENT logo sits bottom-left and the copyright block sits beside it, with the page number '62' bottom-right at about 95.5%; no slide-number field is shown beyond that text.

**Visuals**

Table of five rows with two columns, no header row. Column 1 (label, lime #D5FF79 fill): row 1 number 1 in a dark circle then 'Capex'; row 2 number 2 then 'Dividends'; row 3 number 3 then 'Debt pay down'; row 4 number 4 then 'Share repurchases'; row 5 number 5 then 'M&A'. Column 2 (description, white fill, blue text): row 1 'Expected annual spend between 3-5% of revenue to support investment in organic growth'; row 2 'Increasing each year with underlying earnings growth'; row 3 'Target net debt to adjusted EBITDA less than 2.5x'; row 4 'Opportunistic buy backs'; row 5 'De-emphasized in near term; complement organic growth strategy with M&A over time, as needed'. Numbered markers are filled dark olive-black circles (about #2A2D1E) with lime numerals, each about 2.1% wide by 3.9% tall. Dashed white horizontal rule across the full slide width at about 69.8% of height separating rows 4 and 5. One logo: the white AVIENT wordmark with a stylised diamond-lattice emblem, bottom-left, about 7.5% wide by 3.2% tall (220 by 52 px source).

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF. Solid bright blue background #0095FF; title in white #FFFFFF Arial Bold, sans-serif, about 5.9% of slide height for the title and about 3.0% of slide height for body text (the labels are about 4.4%). Row labels are black #000000 Arial Bold; description text and the dashed rule accent are blue #0095FF on white cells; numerals are lime #D5FF79. Lime cells #D5FF79 and white cells #FFFFFF. Rows have flat fills with no shadow; the outer corners of the table container are rounded with a radius of about 3.5% of slide width, the top-left and top-right of row 1 and the bottom-left and bottom-right of row 5 being rounded. Footer text is ArialMT regular in white at about 1.5% of slide height; page number about 2.2%.

### Slide 62

**Text**

Avient is a compelling investment thesis
Portfolio
aligned to high growth market segments
and secular trends
ORGANIC GROWTH
complemented by M&A over time
as needed
Innovation
differentiation by hybridizing technologies
to create platforms at scale
MARGIN EXPANSION
and sustainable growth
Global reach with local touch
customer-centric focus across continents and cultures
to serve locally and win globally
Broad customer base and
COMPETITIVE ADVANTAGE
Ability to adapt & pivot to deliver results
highly motivated team with track record of
operational and commercial excellence
Strong CASH GENERATION
and EXECUTION
Copyright © 2024 .
Avient Corporation. All Rights Reserved
63

**Layout**

Four horizontal rows stacked in a single column filling the slide below the title, each row with a photo strip at the far left, a white chevron-ended text panel, a connector line and a lime tag at the right; title is top-left, left-aligned, at about 3% left margin and 6% from top; footer with the AVIENT logo bottom-left and copyright text beside it, with the page number '63' bottom-right; no margins between photo strips and the slide edge at left.

**Visuals**

Four photographs in a left column, each about 14.7% wide and 16% tall, sitting flush against the left slide edge. Row 1 (top): hands placing gold coins on stacks over a dark grey hexagonal-pattern surface, grey and gold tones, close framing, full-bleed within its box. Row 2: a hand holding a glowing light bulb with a brain-like filament against a dark navy background with blue and magenta network lines, lit by the bulb glow, cropped to box. Row 3: two pairs of hands cupping a green globe on a blurred green grass background, soft daylight, green dominant, cropped to box. Row 4 (bottom): small green seedlings growing from stacks of gold coins in dark soil against a bright sunlit yellow-green blur, cropped to box. Each row has a white chevron panel with a pointed right end and a thin white horizontal connector line running from the panel tip to a blue-ringed white dot on the left edge of the lime tag. Four lime tags at the right with a diagonal cut at their top-left corner and a fade to teal at the right edge. White AVIENT logo with a stylised diamond-lattice mark at bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0095FF. Solid bright blue background #0095FF with no gradient or pattern. Title in white (#FFFFFF) Arial Bold, sentence case, very large, about 5.9% of slide height. Row headings in blue #0095FF Arial Bold about 3.3% of slide height; row body text in blue #0095FF Arial regular about 2.6%. Tag headings in black #000000 Arial Bold capitals about 3.3%; tag body text in black Arial Bold about 2.6%. Panels white #FFFFFF with sharp corners and angled point at right end. Tags lime green about #D4FF7A with a gradient fading to teal about #7FD9B8 at the right edge; top-left corner cut diagonally. Connector lines white about 0.3% stroke. Connector dots white centre with blue #0095FF ring. Footer text white Arial about 1.5% of slide height; page number white Arial about 2.2%. No shadows.

### Slide 63

**Text**

AVIENT

**Layout**

A single centred composition on a plain white slide: a large circular Earth-imagery emblem sits in the upper-middle area, and the AVIENT logo lockup sits centred below it. There is no title, subtitle, footer or slide number, and the content is centred horizontally with generous white margins on all sides.

**Visuals**

Image 1, an illustration/photograph composite of a circular emblem (about 51% of slide width, 70.7% of slide height, placed at x 24.5, y 10.1) made of satellite Earth imagery cut into an X-shaped pattern of chevron segments separated by white gaps. The segments show Europe and the Mediterranean (green and brown with blue sea), the Arabian Peninsula and Red Sea (tan and gold desert), North Africa (orange-tan Sahara with green patches), the Indian subcontinent (green and brown), and blue ocean with white cloud. Lighting is even, as in satellite imagery, with no shadows. The emblem is not cropped, is not full-bleed, and sits unboxed on white. Dominant colours are ocean blue about #2C6DB5, desert tan about #C9A46A, vegetation green about #4E7A3A and white cloud. Image 2, the AVIENT logo lockup (about 51% by 7.5% box at x 24.5, y 85.3, with the visible mark much smaller and centred): a black icon of interlocking angular X and diamond strokes to the left of the bold condensed black capitals AVIENT, in near-black about #231F20. No other visuals.

**Layout**

A single-column financial reconciliation page on a white portrait sheet with a centered three-line heading at the top, a justified introductory paragraph below it, and a full-width table with a label column on the left (about 8% to 75% of slide width) and two right-aligned numeric columns, 2006 and 2023, grouped under a 'Year Ended December 31,' spanning header. Left and right margins are about 8% of slide width; the table ends near 41% of slide height and the lower half of the page is empty. The title block is centered at the top, and a centered page number '1' sits at the bottom of the page at about 95% of slide height.

**Visuals**

None. The slide contains no charts, photographs, logos or icons. Its only graphic elements are a 4-column data table with horizontal rules and light-blue row banding: rows alternate #CCEEFF and #FFFFFF. Rules are black, about 0.1% of slide height thick: one above the 'Year Ended December 31,' header across the two numeric columns, one under the 2006 and 2023 headers, one above the 'Depreciation and amortization from continuing operations' result line (about 28.8% of slide height), one above 'Adjusted EBITDA' (about 37.7%), and a double rule under the Adjusted EBITDA values (about 39.5%). Table columns: row label; 2006 (values with $ on the first and total rows); 2023 (values with $ on the first and total rows). Table rows with values: Sales $ 2,622.4 / $ 3,142.8; Net income from continuing operations – GAAP $ 133.5 / $ 76.3; Income tax expense 29.7 / 11.0; Interest expense, net 63.1 / 115.3; Depreciation and amortization from continuing operations 57.1 / 188.8; EBITDA from continuing operations 283.4 / 391.4; Special items, before tax (34.0) / 114.6; Interest expense included in special items — / (2.3); Depreciation and amortization included in special items — / (1.9); JV - equity income (107.0) / —; Adjusted EBITDA $ 142.4 / $ 501.8; a blank banded spacer row; Adjusted EBITDA as a percent of sales 5.4 % / 16.0 %. No chart series, legend or highlighted points.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white printed financial page with no decorative treatment. Background #FFFFFF. Headings and body text are black #000000. Table row bands are light blue #CCEEFF, alternating with white #FFFFFF rows. Typeface is Arial (Helvetica-like grotesque sans-serif): bold for the heading lines, the 'Year Ended December 31,' header, the years and the table caption; regular for the paragraph and table body. Title text is about 1.14% of slide height, the same size as body text; the page number is a serif Times New Roman about 1.26%. Rules are thin black lines. No corner radii, shadows or gradients.
